# Detail CRM — database schema (column-level contract)

> Generated by `python3 scripts/gen_types.py` from a throwaway Postgres cluster built with `scripts/test_db.sh` (local Supabase shim + every migration from zero). **Do not edit by hand**: change a migration and regenerate. `python3 scripts/check_contracts.py` fails when this file or `web/src/lib/database.types.ts` is stale, and verifies every table, column, RPC and storage bucket that the web app, the iOS app and the edge functions reference against the same live schema (SPEC §8.2).

- Migrations applied: 111 (`0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql` … `0131_billing_public_offer.sql`), content sha256/16: `f2ec9d17c15ca16d`
- Counts: 99 tables, 0 views, 653 functions (450 non-trigger), 41 enums, 1 composite types, 224 public RLS policies, 5 storage buckets (22 storage.objects policies)
- TypeScript: `web/src/lib/database.types.ts` (same generator run). Swift: models map these names 1:1 through explicit `CodingKeys` under a `// table: <name>` or `// rpc: <name>` annotation.
- Legend: grants `S/I/U/D` = table-level SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE for the PostgREST role (RLS still applies; `service_role` bypasses RLS). `cols UPDATE(a, b)` = column-level grant only. Policy expressions are abbreviated (`public.` stripped, whitespace collapsed, long ones truncated with …). `exec:` = roles with EXECUTE on a function (includes PUBLIC grants). DEFINER = SECURITY DEFINER.
- Called by: the web (`web/src`), iOS (`ios/`) and edge-function (`supabase/functions/<name>`) files that call the function by a static name (the same scan `scripts/check_contracts.py` verifies; tests excluded), pg_cron jobs and other setup SQL in `supabase/setup/`, other public SQL functions (`public.<fn>(` in their body) and RLS policies (public tables and `storage.objects`). Trigger functions list their triggers instead.
- Deviations from `supabase gen types typescript` (opt-in per object, through contract tags in the migrations; everything else is byte-identical to the Supabase generator): a column commented `@insert-optional` — a NOT NULL column without a default that a BEFORE INSERT trigger always fills — is optional in its table's `Insert` type (Supabase makes it required); a function commented `@nullable: a, b` types those RETURNS TABLE output columns `T | null` (Supabase types every RETURNS TABLE column non-null, because Postgres does not record their nullability). Such columns are listed per table / function below.

## Foundation (0001–0009): tenancy, shop setup, CRM, catalog, jobs, scheduling

### Enums

- `business_type`: fixed | mobile | both  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `coupon_kind`: percent | fixed  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `customer_lifecycle`: lead | customer  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `customer_source`: staff | online_booking | referral | google | facebook | instagram | walk_in | other | import  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `deposit_type`: percent | fixed  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `discount_kind`: none | percent | fixed  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `document_kind`: job | quote | invoice  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `job_source`: staff | online_booking | quote | membership  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `job_status`: requested | scheduled | confirmed | en_route | in_progress | completed | cancelled | no_show  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `location_type`: shop | mobile  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `resource_kind`: bay | van | other  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `service_kind`: service | package | addon | product  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_
- `shop_role`: owner | admin | manager | technician  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_

### Composite types

- `document_totals`(subtotal_cents bigint, discount_cents bigint, taxable_subtotal_cents bigint, taxable_discount_cents bigint, tax_cents bigint, total_cents bigint)  _(0001_foundation_extensions_enums_helpers.sql)_ — Result of compute_document_totals (shared by jobs, quotes, invoices).

### Tables

#### `blocked_times`

file `0003_foundation_shop_setup.sql` · also altered/policies in `0050_sched_schema.sql`, `0052_sched_calendar_events.sql`, `0115_sched_event_occurrence_exceptions.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> blocked_times — member_id null blocks the whole shop (and online booking).

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| member_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| starts_at | timestamp with time zone | no |  |  |
| ends_at | timestamp with time zone | no |  |  |
| reason | text | yes |  |  |
| created_by | uuid | yes |  |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| kind | calendar_event_kind | no | 'closed'::calendar_event_kind | closed (shop-wide, blocks online booking) \| time_off (a member) \| meeting \| consultation \| reminder \| other. |
| title | text | yes |  |  |
| customer_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| names_customer | boolean | no | false | True once the event has named a customer (blocked_times_50_validate sets it whenever customer_id is set). It stays true when the link is cleared - deleting the customer sets customer_id null - so the… |
| affects_capacity | boolean | yes |  | Whether the event takes capacity: a shop-wide event counts as one busy unit, a member event makes that member unavailable. Defaults to true for closed / time_off. |
| color | text | yes |  |  |
| recurrence | jsonb | yes |  | Repeat rule {freq, interval, by_weekday, until_date \| count, except_dates} expanded in shop-local wall time (blocked_time_occurrences). except_dates (0115): shop-local start dates of skipped occurren… |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id)
- FK `blocked_times_created_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `blocked_times_customer_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (customer_id)
- FK `blocked_times_member_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, member_id) REFERENCES shop_members(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `blocked_times_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `blocked_times_affects_capacity_set` (affects_capacity IS NOT NULL); `blocked_times_color_check` (color IS NULL OR is_valid_hex_color(color)); `blocked_times_names_customer_set` (customer_id IS NULL OR names_customer); `blocked_times_order` (ends_at > starts_at); `blocked_times_reason_check` (reason IS NULL OR char_length(reason) <= 500); `blocked_times_recurrence_check` (calendar_recurrence_valid(recurrence)); `blocked_times_title_check` (title IS NULL OR char_length(title) <= 120)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SIUD
- Policies:
  - `blocked_times_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `blocked_times_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `blocked_times_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `(is_shop_manager(shop_id) OR (is_shop_member(shop_id) AND ((member_id IS NULL) OR is_own_member(member_id))))`
  - `blocked_times_select_customer_redaction` SELECT to authenticated (RESTRICTIVE) — USING `((NOT names_customer) OR is_shop_manager(shop_id))`
  - `blocked_times_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `blocked_times_10_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `blocked_times_20_set_created_by` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → set_created_by(); `blocked_times_45_keep_exceptions` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → blocked_times_keep_exceptions(); `blocked_times_50_validate` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → blocked_times_validate(); `blocked_times_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

#### `booking_settings`

file `0003_foundation_shop_setup.sql` · also altered/policies in `0050_sched_schema.sql`, `0061_money_schema.sql`, `0081_comms_schema.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> booking_settings — one row per shop (seeded, disabled by default).

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| enabled | boolean | no | false |  |
| auto_confirm | boolean | no | false |  |
| lead_time_minutes | integer | no | 120 |  |
| max_days_ahead | integer | no | 60 |  |
| slot_interval_minutes | integer | no | 30 |  |
| buffer_minutes | integer | no | 0 |  |
| max_concurrent_jobs | integer | no | 1 |  |
| require_deposit | boolean | no | false |  |
| deposit_type | deposit_type | no | 'percent'::deposit_type |  |
| deposit_value | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| service_area_postal_codes | text[] | no | '{}'::text[] |  |
| booking_message | text | yes |  |  |
| cancellation_policy | text | yes |  |  |
| allow_client_cancel_hours | integer | no | 24 |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| max_concurrent_shop | integer | yes |  | At most this many in-shop jobs at once (null = only max_concurrent_jobs applies). |
| max_concurrent_mobile | integer | yes |  | At most this many mobile jobs at once (null = only max_concurrent_jobs applies). |
| count_member_availability | boolean | no | false | Online capacity is also limited by the active, bookable team members not away (time off / events that affect capacity). |
| allow_multi_day | boolean | no | false | A booking longer than the opening it starts in continues at the next openings (up to multi_day_max_days local days). |
| multi_day_max_days | smallint | no | 2 |  |
| quote_self_schedule | boolean | no | false | Customers may pick a time for an approved quote on its /q page (public_quote_slots / public_schedule_quote). |
| meta_pixel_id | text | yes |  | Meta (Facebook) Pixel id loaded on the public booking page. |
| ga4_measurement_id | text | yes |  | Google Analytics 4 measurement id (G-XXXX) loaded on the public booking page. |

- PK: (shop_id)
- FK `booking_settings_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `booking_settings_allow_client_cancel_hours_check` (allow_client_cancel_hours >= 0 AND allow_client_cancel_hours <= 8760); `booking_settings_booking_message_check` (booking_message IS NULL OR char_length(booking_message) <= 5000); `booking_settings_buffer_minutes_check` (buffer_minutes >= 0 AND buffer_minutes <= 480); `booking_settings_cancellation_policy_check` (cancellation_policy IS NULL OR char_length(cancellation_policy) <= 5000); `booking_settings_deposit_percent` (deposit_type <> 'percent'::deposit_type OR deposit_value <= 10000); `booking_settings_deposit_required` (NOT require_deposit OR deposit_value > 0); `booking_settings_deposit_value_check` (deposit_value >= 0); `booking_settings_ga4_measurement_id_check` (ga4_measurement_id IS NULL OR ga4_measurement_id ~ '^G-[A-Z0-9]{4,16}$'); `booking_settings_lead_time_minutes_check` (lead_time_minutes >= 0 AND lead_time_minutes <= 43200); `booking_settings_max_concurrent_jobs_check` (max_concurrent_jobs >= 1 AND max_concurrent_jobs <= 100); `booking_settings_max_concurrent_mobile_check` (max_concurrent_mobile IS NULL OR max_concurrent_mobile >= 1 AND max_concurrent_mobile <= 100); `booking_settings_max_concurrent_shop_check` (max_concurrent_shop IS NULL OR max_concurrent_shop >= 1 AND max_concurrent_shop <= 100); `booking_settings_max_days_ahead_check` (max_days_ahead >= 1 AND max_days_ahead <= 365); `booking_settings_meta_pixel_id_check` (meta_pixel_id IS NULL OR meta_pixel_id ~ '^[0-9]{5,20}$'); `booking_settings_multi_day_max_days_check` (multi_day_max_days >= 2 AND multi_day_max_days <= 7); `booking_settings_service_area_postal_codes_check` (array_position(service_area_postal_codes, NULL::text) IS NULL); `booking_settings_slot_interval_minutes_check` (slot_interval_minutes >= 5 AND slot_interval_minutes <= 240)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SU
- Policies:
  - `booking_settings_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_member(shop_id)`
  - `booking_settings_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_admin(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `booking_settings_10_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `booking_settings_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

#### `business_hours`

file `0003_foundation_shop_setup.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> business_hours — weekday 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday (extract(dow)). Several non-overlapping intervals per day allowed; no row = closed. Times are wall clock in the shop's time zone; closes_at may be 24:00.

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| weekday | smallint | no |  |
| opens_at | time without time zone | no |  |
| closes_at | time without time zone | no |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id)
- FK `business_hours_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- EXCLUDE `business_hours_no_overlap`: EXCLUDE USING gist (shop_id WITH =, weekday WITH =, numrange(EXTRACT(epoch FROM opens_at), EXTRACT(epoch FROM closes_at)) WITH &&)
- CHECK: `business_hours_order` (closes_at > opens_at); `business_hours_weekday_check` (weekday >= 0 AND weekday <= 6)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SIUD
- Policies:
  - `business_hours_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
  - `business_hours_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
  - `business_hours_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_member(shop_id)`
  - `business_hours_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_admin(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `business_hours_10_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `business_hours_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

> coupons — code unique per shop, case-insensitive (citext).

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| value | bigint | no |  |  |
| starts_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| ends_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| max_redemptions | integer | yes |  |  |
| redemptions | integer | no | 0 |  |
| online_only | boolean | no | false |  |
| active | boolean | no | true |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| service_ids | uuid[] | yes |  | Services the coupon discounts (null = every line). Lines of other services are not discount-eligible; a job needs at least one eligible line. |
| min_subtotal_cents | bigint | yes |  | Minimum eligible subtotal (line totals of eligible lines) for the coupon to apply. |
| once_per_customer | boolean | no | false | Each customer may use the coupon on one job (coupon_redemptions). |
| customer_id | uuid | yes |  | Only this customer may use the coupon (null = anyone). |
| new_customers_only | boolean | no | false | Only customers without a completed job or a received payment may use it. |
| referrer_customer_id | uuid | yes |  | Referral coupon of this customer (referral program, P-29). Server-set only. |

- UNIQUE: (shop_id, code); (shop_id, id)

- FK `coupons_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE

| service_postal_code | text | yes |  |  |

| notes | text | yes |  |  |

| discount_kind | discount_kind | no | 'none'::discount_kind |  |
| discount_value | bigint | no | 0 |  |

| discount_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |

| tax_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| total_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| deposit_required_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| public_token | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |
| created_by | uuid | yes |  |  |
| confirmed_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| en_route_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| started_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |

| series_seq | integer | yes |  | Occurrence number within the series (unique per series). |
| series_detached | boolean | no | false | True once the occurrence was edited on its own through the API (time, resource, vehicle, customer, location, address, notes, discount / coupon, line items or assignees): "this and following" series e… |
| custom_data | jsonb | no | '{}'::jsonb | Answers to the shop's job fields / booking questions {key: value} (online bookings store their answers here; validated by the comms range). |
| route_position | integer | yes |  | Manual stop order of the job within its local day (0 = first; set_route_order). |
| sold_by_member_id | uuid | yes |  | Team member credited with the sale (sales commission). Defaults to the creating member (jobs_60_sold_by_default; online bookings: none; quote conversions: the quote's creator; recurring-series occurr… |
| deposit_followups_paused | boolean | no | false | Staff paused the automatic deposit reminders of this job. |

- FK `jobs_quote_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, quote_id) REFERENCES quotes(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (quote_id)

`jobs_schedule_required` (scheduled_start IS NOT NULL OR (status = ANY (ARRAY['requested'::job_status, 'cancelled'::job_status]))); `jobs_series_pair` ((series_id IS NULL) = (series_seq IS NULL)); `jobs_series_seq_check` (series_seq IS NULL OR series_seq >= 1); `jobs_service_city_check` (service_city IS NULL OR char_length(service_city) <= 100); `jobs_service_lat_check` (service_lat IS NULL OR service_lat >= '-90'::integer::double precision AND service_lat <= 90::double precision); `jobs_service_lat_lng_pair` ((service_lat IS NULL) = (service_lng IS NULL)); `jobs_service_lng_check` (service_lng IS NULL OR service_lng >= '-180'::integer::double precision AND service_lng <= 180::double precision); `jobs_service_postal_code_check` (service_postal_code IS NULL OR char_length(service_postal_code) <= 20); `jobs_service_region_check` (service_region IS NULL OR char_length(service_region) <= 100); `jobs_subtotal_cents_check` (subtotal_cents >= 0); `jobs_tax_cents_check` (tax_cents >= 0); `jobs_tax_rate_bps_check` (tax_rate_bps >= 0 AND tax_rate_bps <= 10000); `jobs_total_cents_check` (total_cents >= 0); `jobs_totals_consistent` (total_cents = (subtotal_cents - discount_cents + tax_cents))

A document that starts referencing the vehicle takes only FOR KEY SHARE on it (its composite FK check), and a plain customer_id change takes FOR NO KEY UPDATE (customer_id is in no unique index); those do not conflict, so an owner change and an uncommitted job/quote/line/membership/inspection for the old owner could both commit, leaving the document with another cus…
- `vehicles_normalize()` — invoker · `0004_foundation_crm.sql` · used by vehicles.vehicles_20_normalize — Normalize VIN / plate to upper case without spaces.

## Money (0010–0019): quotes, invoices, payments, cards, memberships, Stripe

### Enums

- `invoice_status`: draft | open | partially_paid | paid | void  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `membership_interval`: month | year | week  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `membership_status`: incomplete | active | past_due | cancelled  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `payment_kind`: deposit | payment | membership  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `payment_method`: card | card_present | cash | check | bank_transfer | other | gift_card | ach_debit | bnpl  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `payment_status`: pending | succeeded | failed | cancelled | refunded | partially_refunded | processing  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_
- `quote_status`: draft | sent | viewed | approved | declined | expired | converted  _(0010_money_quotes.sql)_

### Tables

#### `customer_payment_methods`

file `0011_money_memberships_cards_events.sql` · also altered/policies in `0071_ops_schema.sql`, `0074_ops_merge_customers.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> customer_payment_methods — Stripe PaymentMethod references only (never card numbers). At most one default per customer.

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| customer_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| stripe_payment_method_id | text | no |  |  |
| brand | text | yes |  |  |
| last4 | text | yes |  |  |
| exp_month | smallint | yes |  |  |
| exp_year | smallint | yes |  |  |
| is_default | boolean | no | false |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| stripe_customer_id | text | yes |  | Stripe customer the payment method is attached to (filled from the owning customer on insert). Charge the card on this Stripe customer: after a merge it can differ from customers.stripe_customer_id. |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id); (shop_id, stripe_payment_method_id); customer_payment_methods_one_default_key (shop_id, customer_id) WHERE is_default
- FK `customer_payment_methods_customer_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `customer_payment_methods_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `customer_payment_methods_brand_check` (brand IS NULL OR char_length(brand) >= 1 AND char_length(brand) <= 30); `customer_payment_methods_exp_month_check` (exp_month IS NULL OR exp_month >= 1 AND exp_month <= 12); `customer_payment_methods_exp_year_check` (exp_year IS NULL OR exp_year >= 2000 AND exp_year <= 2100); `customer_payment_methods_last4_check` (last4 IS NULL OR last4 ~ '^[0-9]{4}$'); `customer_payment_methods_stripe_customer_id_check` (stripe_customer_id IS NULL OR stripe_customer_id ~ '^cus_[A-Za-z0-9]+$'); `customer_payment_methods_stripe_payment_method_id_check` (stripe_payment_method_id ~ '^(pm|card|src)_[A-Za-z0-9]+$')
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S
- Policies:
  - `customer_payment_methods_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `customer_payment_methods_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `customer_payment_methods_70_stripe_customer` BEFORE INSERT ROW → customer_payment_methods_ops_stripe_customer(); `customer_payment_methods_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

#### `invoice_line_items`

file `0012_money_invoices_payments.sql` · also altered/policies in `0061_money_schema.sql`, `0116_money_void_edit_wait_for_open_checkouts.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| invoice_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| service_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| vehicle_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| name | text | no |  |  |
| description | text | yes |  |  |
| quantity | numeric(10,2) | no | 1 |  |
| unit_price_cents | bigint | no |  |  |
| discount_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| taxable | boolean | no | true |  |
| sort | integer | no | 0 |  |
| total_cents | bigint | yes | GENERATED: line_total_cents(quantity, unit_price_cents, discount_cents) |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| job_id | uuid | yes |  | The job this line bills (one of the invoice's jobs, invoice_line_items_validate); copied by create_invoice_from_job(s). |
| discount_eligible | boolean | no | true | Whether the invoice's document discount applies to this line (copied from the job line; default true). |
| fee_id | uuid | yes |  |  |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id)
- FK `invoice_line_items_fee_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, fee_id) REFERENCES shop_fees(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (fee_id)
- FK `invoice_line_items_invoice_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, invoice_id) REFERENCES invoices(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `invoice_line_items_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (job_id)
- FK `invoice_line_items_membership_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, membership_id) REFERENCES memberships(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (membership_id)
- FK `invoice_line_items_service_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, service_id) REFERENCES services(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (service_id)
- FK `invoice_line_items_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `invoice_line_items_vehicle_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, vehicle_id) REFERENCES vehicles(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (vehicle_id)
- CHECK: `invoice_line_items_description_check` (description IS NULL OR char_length(description) <= 5000); `invoice_line_items_discount_cents_check` (discount_cents >= 0); `invoice_line_items_name_check` (char_length(btrim(name)) >= 1 AND char_length(btrim(name)) <= 200); `invoice_line_items_quantity_check` (quantity > 0::numeric); `invoice_line_items_unit_price_cents_check` (unit_price_cents >= 0)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SIUD
- Policies:
  - `invoice_line_items_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `invoice_line_items_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `invoice_line_items_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `can_collect_for_invoice(shop_id, invoice_id)`
  - `invoice_line_items_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `invoice_line_items_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `invoice_line_items_10_guard` BEFORE INSERT OR DELETE OR UPDATE ROW → invoice_line_items_guard(); `invoice_line_items_11_open_checkout` BEFORE DELETE OR UPDATE ROW → invoice_line_items_open_checkout_guard(); `invoice_line_items_20_before_write` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → invoice_line_items_before_write(); `invoice_line_items_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at(); `invoice_line_items_touch_invoice` AFTER INSERT OR DELETE OR UPDATE ROW → invoice_line_items_touch_invoice(); `invoice_line_items_validate` AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → invoice_line_items_validate()

#### `memberships`

file `0011_money_memberships_cards_events.sql` · also altered/policies in `0041_integration_events.sql`, `0061_money_schema.sql`, `0069_money_memberships_referrals.sql`, `0103_billing_lapsed_public_sales.sql`, `0108_money_membership_coverage.sql`, `0110_public_join_gift_abuse_limits.sql`, `0111_money_membership_paid_through.sql`, `0125_privacy_erase_customer.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> memberships Status machine: incomplete -> active | past_due | cancelled; active <-> past_due; active | past_due -> cancelled; cancelled is terminal. Staff may only change the vehicle directly; status is Stripe-driven (service_role). Even an incomplete membership may have a live Checkout link in subscription mode (membership_checkout) that the database does not know about, and paying it after the row was deleted or cancelled would start a subscription that bills with no active membership. So staff never delete memberships (they are history) and abandon an incomplete one only through the payments edge function's membership_cancel, which expires its open links (and stops a subscription a link…

| tax_rate_bps | integer | no |  | Defaults to the shop's tax rate when omitted (quotes_integrity). |
| subtotal_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| discount_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| tax_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| total_cents | bigint | no | 0 |  |
| public_token | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |
| sent_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| viewed_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| approved_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| approved_by_name | text | yes |  |  |
| declined_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| declined_reason | text | yes |  |  |
| expired_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| converted_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| converted_job_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| created_by | uuid | yes |  |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| selected_option_id | uuid | yes |  | The option the customer chose (public_respond_quote); staff may preselect one while the quote is a draft. Until then the lowest-sort option is counted. |
| self_schedule | boolean | no | true | Per-quote opt-out of customer self-scheduling (staff-editable). |
| self_scheduled_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  | When the customer scheduled the approved quote on its page (server-set by public_schedule_quote). |
| followups_paused | boolean | no | false | Staff paused the automatic quote follow-ups of this quote. |

- Optional on insert (`@insert-optional`: a BEFORE INSERT trigger fills them): `number`, `tax_rate_bps`
- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (public_token); (shop_id, id); (shop_id, number)
- FK `quotes_converted_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, converted_job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (converted_job_id)
- FK `quotes_created_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `quotes_customer_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `quotes_selected_option_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, selected_option_id) REFERENCES quote_options(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (selected_option_id)
- FK `quotes_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `quotes_vehicle_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, vehicle_id) REFERENCES vehicles(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (vehicle_id)
- CHECK: `quotes_approved_by_name_check` (approved_by_name IS NULL OR char_length(btrim(approved_by_name)) >= 1 AND char_length(btrim(approved_by_name)) <= 200); `quotes_converted_link` (converted_job_id IS NULL OR status = 'converted'::quote_status); `quotes_declined_reason_check` (declined_reason IS NULL OR char_length(declined_reason) <= 1000); `quotes_discount_cents_check` (discount_cents >= 0); `quotes_discount_value` (discount_value >= 0 AND (discount_kind <> 'none'::discount_kind OR discount_value = 0) AND (discount_kind <> 'percent'::discount_kind OR discount_value <= 10000)); `quotes_internal_notes_check` (internal_notes IS NULL OR char_length(internal_notes) <= 20000); `quotes_notes_check` (notes IS NULL OR char_length(notes) <= 20000); `quotes_subtotal_cents_check` (subtotal_cents >= 0); `quotes_tax_cents_check` (tax_cents >= 0); `quotes_tax_rate_bps_check` (tax_rate_bps >= 0 AND tax_rate_bps <= 10000); `quotes_terms_check` (terms IS NULL OR char_length(terms) <= 20000); `quotes_total_cents_check` (total_cents >= 0); `quotes_totals_consistent` (total_cents = (subtotal_cents - discount_cents + tax_cents))
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SIUD
- Policies:
  - `quotes_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `(is_shop_manager(shop_id) AND (status <> 'converted'::quote_status))`
  - `quotes_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `quotes_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `quotes_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `quotes_01_billing_guard` BEFORE INSERT ROW → billing_guard_new_record(); `quotes_02_erased_customer` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF customer_id ROW → customers_erased_link_guard(); `quotes_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `quotes_10_client_guard` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → quotes_client_guard(); `quotes_20_integrity` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → quotes_integrity(); `quotes_30_status_machine` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → quotes_status_machine(); `quotes_40_compute_totals` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → quotes_compute_totals(); `quotes_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at(); `quotes_validate` AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → quotes_validate(); `quotes_zz_integration_response` AFTER UPDATE OF status ROW WHEN (((old.status IS DISTINCT FROM new.status) AND (new.status = ANY (ARRAY['approved'::quote_status, 'declined'::quote_status])))) → quotes_integration_response(); `quotes_zz_money_option_check` AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OF selected_option_id ROW → quotes_money_option_check()

#### `stripe_events`

file `0011_money_memberships_cards_events.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> stripe_events — webhook idempotency ledger (platform-level, no shop_id).

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | text | no |  |
| type | text | no |  |
| account | text | yes |  |
| received_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| last_attempt_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| attempts | integer | no | 1 |
| processed_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |
| error | text | yes |  |

- PK: (id)
- CHECK: `stripe_events_account_check` (account IS NULL OR account ~ '^acct_[A-Za-z0-9]+$'); `stripe_events_attempts_check` (attempts >= 1); `stripe_events_error_check` (error IS NULL OR char_length(error) <= 5000); `stripe_events_id_check` (id ~ '^evt_[A-Za-z0-9]+$'); `stripe_events_type_check` (char_length(type) >= 1 AND char_length(type) <= 100)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=—
- Policies: none (RLS on → no direct access except service_role / SECURITY DEFINER RPCs)

#### `invoice_link_token`

`invoice_link_token(p_invoice_id uuid)` → `uuid`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · stable · exec: authenticated · search_path="" · file `0015_money_privileges.sql`
- invoices.public_token is the customer's credential: whoever holds it can, without signing in, read the invoice (customer name, lines, payments with card brand/last4) and open Checkout for it — i.e. act as the customer. A technician who may collect payments on an assigned job reads that job's invoice (invoices_select) but must not keep a working customer link after the owner turns collecting off or removes them; technicians collect through record_manual_payment and the in-app PaymentSheet instead. So, as for jobs.public_token (0042) and form_submissions.public_token (0023): `authenticated` gets SELECT on every invoices column EXCEPT public_token, and owners/admins/managers fetch it with invoice_link_token(invoice_id) to share the /i link. service_role keeps full access (edge functions resolve tokens); anon has no table access. A migration that adds an invoices column must grant SELECT on it to authenticated (20_form_token_privacy.sql checks the whole column set).
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/invoices/api.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/InvoiceService.swift`

#### `job_payment_summary`

`job_payment_summary(p_job_id uuid)` → `TABLE(job_id uuid, invoice_id uuid, invoice_number bigint, invoice_status invoice_status, total_cents bigint, deposit_required_cents bigint, deposit_paid_cents bigint, deposit_due_cents bigint, paid_cents bigint, tip_cents bigint, refunded_cents bigint, pending_cents bigint, balance_cents bigint, invoice_job_count integer)`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · stable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0013_money_rpcs.sql`, `0063_money_invoice_jobs.sql`
- job_payment_summary(job) — collector. Money picture of one job: paid / deposit_paid / tip net received (refunds subtracted) refunded Σ refunded_cents pending Σ amount + tip of in-flight payments (payment_in_flight) total / balance from the job's non-void invoice when there is one, else job total − paid deposit_due what is still needed to cover the required deposit (any received money counts)
- Nullable output columns (`@nullable`): `invoice_id`, `invoice_number`, `invoice_status`
- Called by:

  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/JobService.swift`
  - SQL functions: `job_series_occurrence_eligible`

#### `money_public_quote_json`

`money_public_quote_json(p_quote_id uuid)` → `jsonb`

- invoker · sql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0014_money_public_rpcs.sql`, `0067_money_quotes_v2.sql`
- money_public_quote_json — curated /q document (0014) plus options and the self-scheduling block: quote.has_options, quote.selected_option_id (the effective option), options [{id, name, description, sort, subtotal_cents, discount_cents, tax_cents, total_cents}], line option_id, self_schedule {available, converted, job_token, deposit_due_cents, payment_pending}: job_token / deposit_due_cents / payment_pending only for a quote the customer scheduled on this page (never for a staff conversion), and only while the job still belongs to the quote's customer (null once staff moved it to someone else). payment_pending = a payment of the job is on its way (a pending card attempt, or an ACH / pay-later payment still 'processing' — in flight for days, payment_in_flight): deposit_due_cents counts only money received, so a client must not ask for the deposit again while it is true (same rule as public_get_booking's deposit.payment_pending). Once the job is on a live invoice (single-job or grouped, through invoice_jobs) the deposit is capped at the invoice's total and never exceeds what the invoice still owes, and a payment in flight on a grouped invoice (its payments carry no job_id) counts as…

#### `record_manual_payment`

`record_manual_payment(p_invoice_id uuid, p_amount_cents bigint, p_method payment_method, p_tip_cents bigint DEFAULT 0, p_note text DEFAULT NULL::text)` → `payments`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0013_money_rpcs.sql`, `0064_money_async_terminal.sql`, `0109_money_cancel_and_open_checkouts.sql`
- record_manual_payment — collector (0013). Only cash / check / bank_transfer / other: cards, ACH debits and pay-later go through Stripe, and gift cards through redeem_gift_card / redeem_customer_credit. A 'processing' (ACH) payment counts as in flight, so it is never paid twice.
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/invoices/api.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/PaymentService.swift`

#### `record_stripe_event`

`record_stripe_event(p_event_id text, p_type text, p_account text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `boolean`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0011_money_memberships_cards_events.sql`
- service_role: webhook idempotency. record_stripe_event returns true when the caller should process the event: it is new, or an earlier attempt failed (error recorded), or an earlier attempt never finished within 5 minutes (crashed worker). Returns false for processed events and for attempts still in flight — so the same event delivered twice has one effect.
- Called by: no caller in this repo (service-role API / manual operations only)

#### `refund_manual_payment`

`refund_manual_payment(p_payment_id uuid, p_amount_cents bigint)` → `payments`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0013_money_rpcs.sql`, `0066_money_gift_cards.sql`
- refund_manual_payment — owner/admin (0013). Stripe-backed methods (card, card_present, ach_debit, bnpl) are refunded through Stripe; a gift card payment's refund goes back onto its card ('refund' transaction). The re-credit is refused (22023, nothing changes) when the card * is void, * was bought online and that purchase was refunded, in full or in part (gift_card_orders.refunded_cents > 0): gift_card_order_refunded took the refund off the unspent balance and reported what was already spent as unrecovered, so crediting the spent value back would hand the buyer that money twice (cash back from Stripe AND spendable credit), or * has expired (expires_at passed): the value would land on a card that can never be redeemed. The card row is locked before its order is read, the same order as gift_card_order_refunded, so a purchase refund and a payment refund of the same card serialize. p_amount_cents comes off the amount first, then tip.
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/invoices/api.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/PaymentService.swift`

#### `void_invoice`

`void_invoice(p_invoice_id uuid, p_reason text DEFAULT NULL::text)` → `invoices`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0013_money_rpcs.sql`, `0063_money_invoice_jobs.sql`, `0116_money_void_edit_wait_for_open_checkouts.sql`
- (COMMENT ON) Owner/admin: void an invoice (reason optional, max 1000). Grouped invoices: every job is locked first (id order, before the invoice — the order payments use), each job payment goes back to its job, and the jobs are released (invoice_jobs.voided, by trigger). 22023 when already void, while a card payment is in progress, or while payments without a job are kept on it (refund them first); job payments go back to their job. 55000 HINT checkout_open while a card pay page of the invoice (or a deposit page of one of its jobs) can still be paid (0116; cancel_open_payments first).
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/invoices/api.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/InvoiceService.swift`

### Functions

#### `apply_checklist_template`

`apply_checklist_template(p_job_id uuid, p_template_id uuid)` → `SETOF job_checklist_items`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0021_field_ops_checklists.sql`
- RPC: apply_checklist_template (manager+). Returns the newly added items.
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/jobs/fieldApi.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/JobOpsService.swift`

#### `can_read_signature_object`

`can_read_signature_object(p_name text)` → `boolean`

- **DEFINER** · sql · stable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`, `0072_ops_job_reports.sql`
- can_read_signature_object (0025) + the report sign-off folder <shop_id>/reports/<token>/: readable by staff who may see the report's token (managers+, and technicians on the job when the shop lets them share reports), since the folder name is the report credential.
- Called by:
  - RLS policies on: `storage.objects`

#### `can_work_job`

`can_work_job(p_shop_id uuid, p_job_id uuid)` → `boolean`

- **DEFINER** · sql · stable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0020_field_ops_enums_helpers.sql`
- can_work_job — the caller is manager+ of the shop, or an active member of the shop assigned to the job; and the job belongs to that shop. This is the "staff on the job" rule for checklists, inspections, photos, forms, job storage objects and job time entries.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `can_read_signature_object`, `clock_in`, `job_completion_blockers`, `publish_job_report`, `set_job_coordinates`, `set_job_photo_visibility`, `set_route_order`, `sign_form_submission`
  - RLS policies on: `documents`, `form_submissions`, `inspections`, `job_checklist_items`, `job_gate_overrides`, `job_photos`, `job_reports`, `storage.objects`

#### `checklist_attach_template`

`checklist_attach_template(p_shop_id uuid, p_job_id uuid, p_template_id uuid)` → `SETOF job_checklist_items`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0021_field_ops_checklists.sql`, `0073_ops_completion_gates.sql`
- checklist_attach_template (0021) — copies the template's required flag.
- Called by:

#### `claim_storage_purge`

`claim_storage_purge(p_limit integer DEFAULT 500, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `TABLE(request_id bigint, bucket_id text, object_name text)`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`
- Worker API (service_role; the storage-purge edge function). claim_storage_purge: up to p_limit object names that are due for removal, oldest request first. A request with nothing left to remove (every object gone or still referenced) is deleted here, which is how requests finish. Requests handed out are leased for 10 minutes (attempts + 1), and locked rows are skipped, so overlapping workers never get the same request.
- Called by:
  - edge:storage-purge: `supabase/functions/storage-purge/index.ts`

#### `form_signer_ip`

`form_signer_ip()` → `inet`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0020_field_ops_enums_helpers.sql`
- Client IP of the current PostgREST request, or null when unavailable or malformed. Never raises. Used as audit evidence (form_submissions.signer_ip), so it must not be a value the client chose: 1. cf-connecting-ip — set (overwritten) by the edge network in front of the Supabase API gateway; 2. x-real-ip — set by the API gateway from the peer it accepted; 3. the RIGHT-most x-forwarded-for hop — the one the last trusted proxy appended. Earlier hops (in particular the first) are whatever the client sent and are never used. A header that is present but not a valid address is skipped.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `create_online_booking`, `form_submission_sign`, `gift_card_order_prepare_core`, `portal_set_email_marketing`, `public_redeem_gift_card`, `public_resubscribe`, `public_submit_lead`, `public_validate_coupon`

The customer's name on file is returned only to the signed-in client linked to that customer (null otherwise): a form token can be reached through a booking token that anyone can mint by typing an existing customer's email into the public booking form, so the token alone proves nothing about who is reading.

#### `storage_path_uuid`

`storage_path_uuid(p_name text, p_index integer)` → `uuid`

- invoker · sql · immutable · exec: anon, authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0020_field_ops_enums_helpers.sql`
- The uuid in folder position p_index (1-based) of an object name, or null when that segment is not a folder (the last segment is the file name) or is not a canonical uuid. Never raises, so it is safe inside RLS policies. Canonical means the lower-case text form Postgres prints (uuid::text): object names are case-sensitive, and the purge queue (0025) matches the folders of deleted shops, jobs and forms by that exact text, so a folder spelled in upper or mixed case must not be accepted by any storage policy or path validator (its files would never be purged).
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `can_read_signature_object`, `documents_validate`, `inspection_marks_queue_storage_purge`, `inspection_marks_validate`, `inspections_validate`, `is_signed_evidence`, `job_photos_validate`, `public_form_signature_upload_allowed`, `public_report_signature_upload_allowed`, `queue_storage_purge`, `storage_object_in_use`
  - RLS policies on: `storage.objects`
  - CHECK constraints / column defaults of: `storage_purge_requests`

### Trigger functions

- `checklist_templates_normalize()` — invoker · `0021_field_ops_checklists.sql` · used by checklist_templates.checklist_templates_20_normalize — Normalize items: generate missing ids, trim labels. Anything that is not the expected shape is left untouched for the CHECK to reject.
- `form_submissions_before_insert()` — invoker · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by form_submissions.form_submissions_10_before_insert — Direct inserts (managers+): everything but the job and template is filled by the server. SECURITY INVOKER: the template and job are read with the caller's own RLS. Missing/foreign templates are left for RLS (which runs before NOT NULL/CHECK), NOT NULL and the composite FKs to reject, so unauthorized callers get 42501.
- `form_submissions_client_guard()` — invoker · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by form_submissions.form_submissions_10_client_guard — Signed submissions are permanent for API callers.
- `form_submissions_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql` · used by form_submissions.form_submissions_queue_storage_purge
- `form_templates_normalize()` — invoker · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by form_templates.form_templates_20_normalize
- `inspection_marks_client_guard()` — invoker · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by inspection_marks.inspection_marks_10_client_guard — Direct writes: marks of a signed inspection are locked. The parent is read with the caller's own RLS; a parent the caller cannot see is rejected by the marks policies anyway.
- `inspection_marks_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql` · used by inspection_marks.inspection_marks_queue_storage_purge — A mark's photo: queued when the mark goes with its inspection (the inspection row is already gone when the cascaded mark deletion runs). A mark deleted on its own keeps its photo (the app removes it through the Storage API, or it stays in the job's gallery); a mark removed with its job or shop is covered by that folder's request.
- `inspection_marks_validate()` — **DEFINER** · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by inspection_marks.inspection_marks_validate — AFTER: the mark photo belongs to the inspection's job folder and exists.
- `inspections_client_guard()` — invoker · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by inspections.inspections_10_client_guard — Direct writes: sign/un-sign rules and the signed lock.
- `inspections_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql` · used by inspections.inspections_queue_storage_purge
- `inspections_validate()` — **DEFINER** · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql` · used by inspections.inspections_validate — AFTER: the vehicle belongs to the job's customer (or is a vehicle on one of the job's line items); a new signature image exists under the shop folder.
- `job_checklist_items_client_guard()` — invoker · `0021_field_ops_checklists.sql`, `0073_ops_completion_gates.sql` · used by job_checklist_items.job_checklist_items_10_client_guard — job_checklist_items_client_guard (0021) — technicians still only tick and untick; the required flag is named explicitly (managers set it).
- `job_line_items_attach_checklists()` — **DEFINER** · `0021_field_ops_checklists.sql` · used by job_line_items.job_line_items_attach_checklists — AFTER INSERT / UPDATE OF service_id on job_line_items.
- `job_photos_client_guard()` — invoker · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql`, `0076_ops_job_media.sql` · used by job_photos.job_photos_10_client_guard — job_photos_client_guard (0022) — + media columns are fixed after insert.
- `job_photos_validate()` — **DEFINER** · `0022_field_ops_inspections_photos.sql`, `0076_ops_job_media.sql` · used by job_photos.job_photos_validate — job_photos_validate (0022) — the object exists in the row's bucket; video names start with 'v-'; the poster is a JPEG of the same job folder.
- `jobs_attach_forms()` — **DEFINER** · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_attach_forms — Jobs → forms: attach on insert; unsigned forms follow the job's customer.
- `jobs_customer_records_guard()` — **DEFINER** · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql`, `0074_ops_merge_customers.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_customer_records_guard — jobs_customer_records_guard (0023) — merge bypass.
- `jobs_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`, `0076_ops_job_media.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_queue_storage_purge
- `jobs_sync_form_customer()` — **DEFINER** · `0023_field_ops_forms.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_sync_form_customer — The previous customer's public upload folder <shop_id>/forms/<old token>/ may already hold a signature image they drew but never submitted. Nothing can reference it any more (the old link is dead and an unsigned form has no signature_path), so it is queued for the storage purge (0025) now; otherwise the customer's image would outlive the form and the job.
- `shop_members_close_time_entries()` — **DEFINER** · `0024_field_ops_time_entries.sql` · used by shop_members.shop_members_50_close_time_entries — Deactivation closes open entries (see the header). AFTER UPDATE OF active on shop_members, every context. An entry whose clock-in lies ahead of the deactivation (a manager set a later time) closes as a zero-length entry.
- `shops_queue_storage_purge()` — **DEFINER** · `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`, `0076_ops_job_media.sql` · used by shops.shops_queue_storage_purge — shops_queue_storage_purge / jobs_queue_storage_purge (0025) — + the range's buckets and folders. (A job's report signature folders are queued by job_reports_zz_ops_queue_storage_purge, 0072: the reports are gone by the time the job's AFTER DELETE trigger runs.)
- `time_entries_client_guard()` — invoker · `0024_field_ops_time_entries.sql` · used by time_entries.time_entries_10_client_guard

## Communication (0030–0039): templates, messages, automations, campaigns, notifications

### Enums

- `campaign_status`: draft | launched | cancelled  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_
- `message_channel`: sms | email  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_
- `message_direction`: outbound | inbound  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_
- `message_status`: queued | sending | sent | delivered | failed | received | cancelled  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_ — 'cancelled' (beyond SPEC §4.7's list): queued messages withdrawn before sending (cancelled campaigns, recipients who opted out after queueing).
- `message_template_key`: booking_request_received | booking_confirmed | appointment_reminder | on_the_way | job_started | job_completed | quote_sent | invoice_sent | payment_receipt | review_request | follow_up | membership_welcome | invite | gift_card_delivery | referral_reward | job_report | quote_reminder | deposit_reminder | invoice_reminder | invoice_overdue | service_followup | lead_received  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_
- `notification_kind`: new_booking | booking_cancelled | quote_approved | quote_declined | payment_received | inbound_message | form_signed | general | gift_card_purchased | membership_joined | low_stock | inspection_acknowledged | job_assigned | job_rescheduled | new_lead | task_assigned | task_due | sms_number_status | webhook_failing | billing_payment_failed  _(0030_comms_enums_config.sql)_

| processed_at | timestamp with time zone | no |  |  |
| outcome | text | no |  | queued (message_ids holds what was queued), skipped (nothing to send: superseded, no consent, template off, …) or no_postal_address (0129: a marketing email the customer had consented to was held bac… |

- FK `job_automation_log_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE

- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S + cols UPDATE(read_at)
- Policies:
  - `messages_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `messages_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`

#### `app_url`

`app_url(p_path text)` → `text`

- invoker · sql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0030_comms_enums_config.sql`

- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `calendar_feed_events`, `comms_customer_vars`, `comms_document_vars`, `comms_invoice_vars`, `comms_job_vars`, `comms_quote_vars`, `enqueue_document_followups`, `enqueue_document_message`, `enqueue_due_automations`, `enqueue_message_core`, `enqueue_service_followups`, `enqueue_template_message`, `launch_campaign`, `payments_integration_succeeded`, `preview_campaign_message`, `preview_document_message`, `publish_job_report`, `referral_code_core`

#### `can_read_notification`

`can_read_notification(p_shop_id uuid, p_kind notification_kind)` → `boolean`

- **DEFINER** · sql · stable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0031_comms_notifications.sql`
- True when the caller is an active member of p_shop_id whose current role may read notifications of p_kind.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `mark_all_notifications_read`
  - RLS policies on: `notifications`

#### `cancel_campaign`

`cancel_campaign(p_campaign_id uuid)` → `campaigns`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0035_comms_campaigns.sql`
- cancel_campaign (manager+): a draft is cancelled outright; a launched campaign has its not-yet-sent messages withdrawn (already sent ones stay). A message in flight at that moment is sent at most once: if the provider asks for a retry, mark_message_result cancels it instead of re-queueing (and the claim never hands out a cancelled campaign's messages).
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/campaigns/api.ts`

#### `claim_queued_messages`

`claim_queued_messages(p_limit integer DEFAULT 50, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `TABLE(id uuid, shop_id uuid, channel message_channel, to_address text, from_address text, subject text, body text, attempts integer, shop_name text, reply_to text, customer_id uuid, job_id uuid, campaign_id uuid, template_key message_template_key, unsubscribe_token uuid, messaging_service_sid text)`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0033_comms_messages.sql`, `0089_comms_sms_webhooks.sql`, `0102_billing_enforcement.sql`
- claim_queued_messages (0089): a lapsed shop's campaign messages stay queued.
- Nullable output columns (`@nullable`): `campaign_id`, `customer_id`, `from_address`, `job_id`, `messaging_service_sid`, `reply_to`, `subject`, `template_key`, `unsubscribe_token`
- Called by:
  - edge:messaging: `supabase/functions/messaging/deliver.ts`

- render_template — pure; byte-for-byte equivalent to renderTemplate() in supabase/functions/_shared/templates.ts: * placeholder = "{{", optional spaces/tabs, name [A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*, optional spaces/tabs, "}}"; names are case-sensitive * strings verbatim, numbers in JSON form, booleans true/false * unknown names, JSON null, objects and arrays render as '' * single pass: substituted values are never re-scanned * anything else (e.g. "{{ a b }}", "{x}") is left untouched Never raises: a null body renders null, non-object vars count as {}.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `comms_render_parts`, `launch_campaign`, `preview_campaign_message`, `preview_document_message`, `preview_template_message`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0033_comms_messages.sql`
- Delivery callbacks (Twilio status webhook / email events) keyed by the provider's message id. Status only moves forward: sending → sent → delivered; sending|sent → failed; failed → delivered Anything else (unknown id, stale or duplicate callback) is ignored. Returns the message id, or null when no message has that provider id.
- Called by:
  - edge:messaging: `supabase/functions/messaging/twilio_webhooks.ts`

## Integration & reports (0040–0049): pricing, events, online booking, portal, realtime, reports, search

### Tables

#### `integration_events`

file `0041_integration_events.sql` · also altered/policies in `0089_comms_sms_webhooks.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Idempotency ledger for cross-domain side effects (0041). Written only by definer code; managers+ may read it.

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| event | text | no |  |
| job_id | uuid | yes |  |
| payment_id | uuid | yes |  |
| form_submission_id | uuid | yes |  |
| membership_id | uuid | yes |  |
| subject_id | uuid | yes | GENERATED: COALESCE(job_id, payment_id, form_submission_id, membership_id) |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (event, subject_id); (shop_id, id)
- FK `integration_events_form_submission_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, form_submission_id) REFERENCES form_submissions(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `integration_events_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `integration_events_membership_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, membership_id) REFERENCES memberships(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `integration_events_payment_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, payment_id) REFERENCES payments(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `integration_events_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `integration_events_event_check` (event = ANY (ARRAY['booking_created', 'booking_confirmed', 'on_the_way', 'job_completed', 'payment_succeeded', 'form_signed', 'membership_activated'])); `integration_events_one_subject` (num_nonnulls(job_id, payment_id, form_submission_id, membership_id) = 1); `integration_events_subject_kind` ( CASE event WHEN 'payment_succeeded' THEN payment_id IS NOT NULL WHEN 'form_signed' THEN form_submission_id IS NOT NULL WHEN 'membership_activated' THEN membership_id IS NOT NULL ELSE job_id IS NOT NULL END)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S
- Policies:
  - `integration_events_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `integration_events_zz_comms_webhooks` AFTER INSERT ROW → integration_events_comms_webhooks()

#### `booking_parse_start`

`booking_parse_start(p_raw text, p_tz text)` → `timestamp with time zone`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0042_integration_public_booking.sql`
- Reads a booking start (see the header): an ISO-8601 date-time with an offset is that instant; one without an offset is wall-clock time in p_tz. 22023 for anything else, and for local times that do not exist or are ambiguous in p_tz (DST changes). Transitions are never closer than a few days apart, so the offsets in effect one day either side are the only candidates.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `create_online_booking_core`, `public_schedule_quote`, `public_validate_coupon`

#### `booking_public_json`

`booking_public_json(p_job_id uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone)` → `jsonb`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0042_integration_public_booking.sql`, `0064_money_async_terminal.sql`
- booking_public_json (integration 0042, owner sched) — deposit.payment_pending also covers a payment still 'processing' (ACH / pay-later). Redefined here (fix forward) instead of editing the committed 0042: a database that already applied 0042 only receives the change through a later file. The rest of the builder is 0042's, unchanged except for grouped invoices (P-7, 0063; sched's fix, made in this redefinition because it is the one that is live): the job's live invoice is found through invoice_jobs, so a job on a grouped invoice (invoices.job_id null, whole-invoice payments without job_id) shows the invoice's paid / balance, a payment in flight on that invoice counts as pending, and a deposit is never due beyond what the invoice still owes. Grants stay as 0042 set them (service_role only; CREATE OR REPLACE keeps them).
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `public_cancel_booking`, `public_get_booking`

#### `price_services_core`

`price_services_core(p_shop_id uuid, p_customer_id uuid, p_vehicle_category_id uuid, p_service_ids uuid[], p_vehicle_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_apply_membership boolean DEFAULT true, p_starts_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT NULL::timestamp with time zone)` → `jsonb`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0040_integration_helpers_pricing.sql`, `0069_money_memberships_referrals.sql`, `0108_money_membership_coverage.sql`
- price_services_core — INTERNAL (no caller checks; used by price_services, create_online_booking and public_validate_coupon). For each service id (de-duplicated, input order kept) the catalog price and duration for the vehicle category (category row, else base row — service_price_for). When p_apply_membership, the customer's ACTIVE memberships apply: shop-wide ones, plus vehicle-scoped ones whose vehicle is p_vehicle_id. A service in any applicable plan's included_service_ids is priced 0 with a note naming the plan; the largest plan discount_bps is returned as the suggested document discount (percent). Callers decide whether a coupon replaces it. Result: { vehicle_category_id, tax_rate_bps, duration_minutes, priced, lines: [{service_id, name, kind, taxable, duration_minutes, catalog_price_cents, unit_price_cents, membership_included, note}], memberships: [{membership_id, plan_id, plan_name, discount_bps, vehicle_id}], suggested_discount_kind ('none'|'percent'), suggested_discount_value, totals: {subtotal_cents, discount_cents, tax_cents, total_cents} | null } priced = every line has a price (a missing catalog price is null and the totals are null). Every service must be an active,…
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `create_online_booking_core`, `job_series_price_lines`, `price_services`, `public_validate_coupon`

## Scheduling v2 (0050–0059): recurring series, calendar events, capacity, booking links, iCal feeds

### Enums

- `calendar_event_kind`: closed | time_off | meeting | consultation | reminder | other  _(0050_sched_schema.sql)_ — blocked_times.kind: closed = the shop is closed (shop-wide only); time_off = a member is away (member only); meeting / consultation / reminder / other = calendar events (consultation and reminder may name a customer).

### Tables

#### `booking_links`

file `0050_sched_schema.sql` · also altered/policies in `0053_sched_slots_links.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Private booking links (?link=<token>): the booking page offers only these services (non-online-bookable ones included). Prices come from the catalog.

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| token | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() | Unguessable public credential of the link (server-issued). |
| name | text | no |  |  |
| service_ids | uuid[] | no |  | Services / packages / add-ons offered through the link (1..50, active services of the shop). |
| note | text | yes |  |  |
| active | boolean | no | true |  |
| expires_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| created_by | uuid | yes |  |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id); (token)
- FK `booking_links_created_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `booking_links_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `booking_links_name_check` (char_length(btrim(name)) >= 1 AND char_length(btrim(name)) <= 120); `booking_links_note_check` (note IS NULL OR char_length(note) <= 1000); `booking_links_service_ids_check` (array_position(service_ids, NULL::uuid) IS NULL AND cardinality(service_ids) >= 1 AND cardinality(service_ids) <= 50)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SIUD
- Policies:
  - `booking_links_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
  - `booking_links_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
  - `booking_links_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `booking_links_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_admin(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `booking_links_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `booking_links_20_set_created_by` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → set_created_by(); `booking_links_50_validate` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → booking_links_validate(); `booking_links_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

#### `calendar_feed_tokens`

file `0050_sched_schema.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Per-member iCal feed credentials (0055). At most one live (unrevoked) token per member; include_all (managers+) lists every job of the shop.

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| member_id | uuid | no |  |
| token | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| include_all | boolean | no | false |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| revoked_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |
| last_accessed_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id); (token); calendar_feed_tokens_live_key (member_id) WHERE (revoked_at IS NULL)
- FK `calendar_feed_tokens_member_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, member_id) REFERENCES shop_members(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `calendar_feed_tokens_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S
- Policies:
  - `calendar_feed_tokens_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `(is_own_member(member_id) AND is_shop_member(shop_id))`
- Triggers: `calendar_feed_tokens_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change()

#### `job_series`

file `0050_sched_schema.sql` · also altered/policies in `0051_sched_job_series.sql`, `0102_billing_enforcement.sql`, `0125_privacy_erase_customer.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Recurring appointment (0051): rule + defaults; occurrences are jobs with series_id/series_seq. RPC-only writes (create_job_series, update_job_series, end_job_series, delete_job_series); managers+ read.

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |

| customer_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| vehicle_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| location_type | location_type | no | 'shop'::location_type |  |
| service_address_line1 | text | yes |  |  |
| service_address_line2 | text | yes |  |  |
| service_city | text | yes |  |  |
| service_region | text | yes |  |  |
| service_postal_code | text | yes |  |  |
| service_lat | double precision | yes |  |  |
| service_lng | double precision | yes |  |  |
| resource_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| freq | text | no |  |  |
| interval | smallint | no | 1 |  |
| by_weekday | smallint[] | no | '{}'::smallint[] |  |
| month_mode | text | yes |  |  |
| month_day | smallint | yes |  |  |
| month_nth | smallint | yes |  |  |
| month_weekday | smallint | yes |  |  |
| start_date | date | no |  |  |
| seq_offset | integer | no | 0 | Occurrence numbers used before start_date (0 for a new series): a rule change re-anchors start_date at the edit point and sets this to the last number before it, so series_seq stays the visit's ordin… |
| local_start | time without time zone | no |  |  |
| duration_minutes | integer | no |  |  |
| until_date | date | yes |  |  |
| max_occurrences | integer | yes |  |  |
| template_lines | jsonb | no | '[]'::jsonb | Services of every occurrence: [{service_id, quantity}] (1..30), priced from the catalog when each occurrence is generated. |
| assignee_member_ids | uuid[] | no | '{}'::uuid[] |  |
| notes | text | yes |  |  |
| internal_notes | text | yes |  |  |
| active | boolean | no | true |  |
| generated_through | date | yes |  | Local date through which occurrences have been generated (generate_series_jobs extends it to the horizon). |
| skipped_seqs | integer[] | no | '{}'::integer[] | Occurrence numbers whose job was deleted outside the series RPCs (a skipped visit): never generated again. Cleared when a rule change renumbers the occurrences. |
| skipped_dates | date[] | no | '{}'::date[] | Shop-local dates of skipped (deleted, not detached) occurrences: no occurrence is generated on them, also after a rule change. |
| held_dates | date[] | no | '{}'::date[] | Shop-local dates (one entry per occurrence) of occurrences a rule change kept although the new rule does not produce their date: they are numbered with the rule's dates (series_seq stays the ordinal,… |
| ended_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |  |
| created_by | uuid | yes |  |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id)
- FK `job_series_created_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `job_series_customer_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
- FK `job_series_resource_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, resource_id) REFERENCES resources(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (resource_id)
- FK `job_series_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `job_series_vehicle_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, vehicle_id) REFERENCES vehicles(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (vehicle_id)
- CHECK: `job_series_assignees` (array_position(assignee_member_ids, NULL::uuid) IS NULL AND cardinality(assignee_member_ids) <= 10); `job_series_by_weekday_check` (is_valid_weekday_set(by_weekday)); `job_series_duration_minutes_check` (duration_minutes >= 15 AND duration_minutes <= 44640); `job_series_ended` (active OR ended_at IS NOT NULL); `job_series_freq_check` (freq = ANY (ARRAY['week', 'month'])); `job_series_held` (array_position(held_dates, NULL::date) IS NULL AND cardinality(held_dates) <= 1000); `job_series_internal_notes_check` (internal_notes IS NULL OR char_length(internal_notes) <= 20000); `job_series_interval_check` ("interval" >= 1 AND "interval" <= 12); `job_series_lat_lng_pair` ((service_lat IS NULL) = (service_lng IS NULL)); `job_series_max_occurrences_check` (max_occurrences IS NULL OR max_occurrences >= 1 AND max_occurrences <= 500); `job_series_month_day_check` (month_day IS NULL OR month_day >= 1 AND month_day <= 31); `job_series_month_mode_check` (month_mode IS NULL OR (month_mode = ANY (ARRAY['day_of_month', 'nth_weekday']))); `job_series_month_nth_check` (month_nth IS NULL OR (month_nth = ANY (ARRAY[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, '-1'::integer]))); `job_series_month_rule` (freq <> 'month' OR cardinality(by_weekday) = 0 AND month_mode IS NOT NULL AND (month_mode <> 'day_of_month' OR month_day IS NOT NULL AND month_nth IS NULL AND month_weekday IS NULL) AND (month_mode <> 'nth_weekday' OR…; `job_series_month_weekday_check` (month_weekday IS NULL OR month_weekday >= 0 AND month_weekday <= 6); `job_series_notes_check` (notes IS NULL OR char_length(notes) <= 20000); `job_series_seq_offset_check` (seq_offset >= 0 AND seq_offset <= 100000); `job_series_service_address_line1_check` (service_address_line1 IS NULL OR char_length(service_address_line1) <= 200); `job_series_service_address_line2_check` (service_address_line2 IS NULL OR char_length(service_address_line2) <= 200); `job_series_service_city_check` (service_city IS NULL OR char_length(service_city) <= 100); `job_series_service_lat_check` (service_lat IS NULL OR service_lat >= '-90'::integer::double precision AND service_lat <= 90::double precision); `job_series_service_lng_check` (service_lng IS NULL OR service_lng >= '-180'::integer::double precision AND service_lng <= 180::double precision); `job_series_service_postal_code_check` (service_postal_code IS NULL OR char_length(service_postal_code) <= 20); `job_series_service_region_check` (service_region IS NULL OR char_length(service_region) <= 100); `job_series_skipped` (array_position(skipped_seqs, NULL::integer) IS NULL AND array_position(skipped_dates, NULL::date) IS NULL); `job_series_template_shape` (jsonb_typeof(template_lines) = 'array'); `job_series_week_rule` (freq <> 'week' OR cardinality(by_weekday) >= 1 AND month_mode IS NULL AND month_day IS NULL AND month_nth IS NULL AND month_weekday IS NULL)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S
- Policies:
  - `job_series_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `job_series_01_billing_guard` BEFORE INSERT ROW → billing_guard_new_record(); `job_series_02_erased_customer` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF customer_id ROW → customers_erased_link_guard(); `job_series_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `job_series_50_validate` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → job_series_validate(); `job_series_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

#### `end_job_series`

`end_job_series(p_series_id uuid, p_after_date date, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0051_sched_job_series.sql`
- end_job_series(series, after_date, p_now) — no occurrences after p_after_date (shop-local date). It only ever shortens a series: the end becomes the earlier of p_after_date and the current until_date (a later date never moves until_date out, which would generate more visits — update_job_series with until_date extends a series). Eligible occurrences after the end are deleted, the others kept (and counted). The series stops (active false) when that end is today or earlier. Returns {deleted, kept}.
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/jobs/seriesApi.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/JobsSeriesService.swift`

#### `job_series_for_edit`

`job_series_for_edit(p_series_id uuid)` → `job_series`

- invoker · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0051_sched_job_series.sql`
- Loads a series for an edit RPC: P0002 unless the caller is an active member of its shop, 42501 below manager. Advisory-locked, then every job of the series is row-locked (id order) and only then the series row: the order a job delete takes them (the job row, then jobs_zz_series_skip updates the series row), so deleting one visit while an edit, the end or the removal of its series runs waits instead of deadlocking (40P01). A visit deleted while this waited is gone and its skip is on the row read here; a delete that comes later waits for this transaction.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `delete_job_series`, `end_job_series`, `update_job_series`

#### `job_series_generate`

`job_series_generate(p_series_id uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone, p_strict boolean)` → `integer`

- invoker · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0051_sched_job_series.sql`
- job_series_generate — extends one series to its horizon (see the header). Returns the number of jobs created. Serialized per series by an advisory lock (the same key the edit RPCs take). Each occurrence is priced at its own start (job_series_price_lines). When an occurrence cannot be priced (non-strict: the cron) generation stops before it and resumes from its date on a later run.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `create_job_series`, `generate_series_jobs`, `update_job_series`

#### `job_series_lock_occurrence`

`job_series_lock_occurrence(p_job_id uuid, p_series_id uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone, OUT o_status text, OUT o_seq integer, OUT o_start timestamp with time zone)` → `record`

- invoker · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0051_sched_job_series.sql`
- job_series_lock_occurrence — locks the job row (waiting for a concurrent confirmation / reschedule / payment to commit) and says, on the locked, current row, whether it is still an eligible occurrence of p_series_id. o_status: 'eligible' | 'kept' | 'gone' (deleted or moved out of the series meanwhile); o_seq / o_start describe the row as it is. job_series_remove_occurrence — the one place the series RPCs delete an occurrence: locks and re-checks it (above), then deletes it in a subtransaction: a delete refused by a foreign key keeps the job (o_status 'kept'). The delete runs with detailcrm.series_rebuild = the series id, so jobs_zz_series_skip does not record it as a skipped visit.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `job_series_remove_occurrence`, `update_job_series`

#### `job_series_price_lines`

`job_series_price_lines(p_series job_series, p_strict boolean, p_starts_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT NULL::timestamp with time zone)` → `jsonb`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0051_sched_job_series.sql`
- job_series_price_lines — the occurrence's line items priced from the catalog now: {lines: [{service_id, name, description, quantity, unit_price_cents, taxable, duration_minutes, membership_id}], discount_value (membership percent in bps), duration_minutes (sum of duration x quantity)}. p_starts_at: the start of the occurrence being priced — membership uses are counted in ITS billing period (null = now, for validation and durations only; occurrences are always written with their own start). An included service is free (unit price 0, membership_id set) only while that period has a use left; the next template line of the same service is charged at the catalog price. p_strict: every template service must be available and priced (22023); otherwise unavailable / unpriced services are left out and null is returned when nothing is left.
- Called by:
