#### `job_series_update_occurrence`

`job_series_update_occurrence(p_s job_series, p_job_id uuid, p_d date, p_tz text, p_now timestamp with time zone, p_reprice boolean, p_reassign boolean, p_old_internal_notes text)` → `boolean`

- invoker · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0051_sched_job_series.sql`
- job_series_update_occurrence — brings an eligible occurrence (locked by the caller) up to the series defaults IN PLACE on its own local date p_d: same job, id, number, public link, automation log and forms. Lines are re-priced (at the occurrence's start) only when p_reprice (the services or the vehicle changed); assignees are rewritten only when p_reassign. Internal notes take the new default only while the job's note is still the series' previous default (p_old_internal_notes): a note written on the visit itself (a technician's field note) is kept. Coordinates: the series' point when it has one; otherwise the job's own point while its address stays the same (null once it moves). Returns false (nothing written) when the new start would already have passed.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `update_job_series`

#### `job_series_write_lines`

`job_series_write_lines(p_s job_series, p_job_id uuid, p_pr jsonb)` → `void`

- invoker · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0051_sched_job_series.sql`
- job_series_write_lines — (re)writes an occurrence's line items and its membership percent discount from p_pr (job_series_price_lines for THIS occurrence's start; a free line carries its membership). Existing service lines are deleted first (their membership uses are released before the new lines take them again). Fee lines (fee_id: added by jobs_zz_money_auto_fees when the occurrence is inserted or its location type changes, or by hand through add_fee_line, money 0068) are kept, so a recurring visit carries the same fees as a one-off job. Without money 0061 (see the header) lines have neither fee_id nor membership_id. job_series_write_assignees — makes the occurrence's assignees exactly the series' members that are still active.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `job_series_insert_occurrence`, `job_series_update_occurrence`

#### `update_job_series`

`update_job_series(p_series_id uuid, p_patch jsonb, p_from_job_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0051_sched_job_series.sql`
- update_job_series(series, patch, from_job, p_now) — "this and following". p_patch keys: vehicle_id, location_type, service address fields, service_lat/lng, resource_id, local_start, duration_minutes, template_lines, assignee_member_ids, notes, internal_notes, freq, interval, by_weekday, month_mode, month_day, month_nth, month_weekday, until_date, max_occurrences. p_from_job_id: an occurrence of this series (null = from the earliest occurrence that has not started yet). The edit point is a date (see the header): the occurrence's date in the current numbering (its original rule date, also when it was moved), or its own date when the current rule does not number it; without p_from_job_id the earliest of the first upcoming occurrence's own and rule dates — for a rule change, the current rule's first date on or after that (or an attached upcoming occurrence's held date, if earlier), so an every-N-weeks / months cadence keeps its phase when that visit was moved into an off-period. Occurrences before that date are never touched. * Without a rule change every eligible occurrence from the edit point on is updated IN PLACE on its own date with the new defaults (new local_start, duration, se…
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/jobs/seriesApi.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/JobsSeriesService.swift`

### Trigger functions

- `blocked_times_validate()` — invoker · `0052_sched_calendar_events.sql` · used by blocked_times.blocked_times_50_validate
- `booking_links_validate()` — invoker · `0053_sched_slots_links.sql` · used by booking_links.booking_links_50_validate — booking_links_50_validate — services: 1..50 distinct active, non-archived services / packages / add-ons of the shop (products are never booked online). API roles: the token is server-issued and never changes.
- `job_series_validate()` — invoker · `0051_sched_job_series.sql` · used by job_series.job_series_50_validate — job_series_50_validate — BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE on job_series (invoker: writes come from definer RPCs, so it reads every row). References are checked when they are written, so later catalog / team changes never block unrelated updates (generation skips what is no longer available).
- `jobs_route_geo_reset()` — **DEFINER** · `0057_sched_routes.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_56_route_geo_reset — jobs_56_route_geo_reset — see the header. Definer only so the shop's time zone is readable whoever writes the job; it changes nothing but the row being written.
- `jobs_series_detach_on_child_edit()` — invoker · `0051_sched_job_series.sql` · used by job_assignments.job_assignments_50_series_detach, job_line_items.job_line_items_50_series_detach — jobs_series_detach_on_child_edit — AFTER INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE on job_line_items and job_assignments (invoker, so it sees the real caller). A line added, removed or repriced, or an assignee changed, through the API is a "this job only" edit of that visit: its occurrence is detached, so a later series edit never re-prices it (dropping an extra the customer asked for, or its price) or resets its…
- `jobs_series_guard()` — invoker · `0051_sched_job_series.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_50_series_guard — jobs_50_series_guard — BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE on jobs (invoker, so it sees the real caller). API roles cannot put a job in a series or move it between series; editing an occurrence's time, resource, vehicle, customer, location type, service address, notes, discount or coupon through the API detaches it ("this job only"), so a later series edit never overwrites or deletes it (line items and assig…
- `jobs_series_record_skip()` — **DEFINER** · `0051_sched_job_series.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_zz_series_skip — jobs_zz_series_skip — AFTER DELETE on jobs (definer: job_series is RPC-only). An occurrence deleted outside the series RPCs is a skipped visit: its number and its RULE date are recorded on the series so no later generation or edit re-creates it. The rule date is the date the current numbering gives its series_seq — also for a detached visit (one edited on its own, often still on that date, or mov…
- `shop_members_bookable_guard()` — invoker · `0053_sched_slots_links.sql` · used by shop_members.shop_members_50_bookable_guard — shop_members_50_bookable_guard — API roles: only owners/admins change bookable (members cannot change their own). shop_members_client_guard (0002) is unchanged.
- `shop_members_revoke_calendar_feed()` — **DEFINER** · `0055_sched_calendar_feeds.sql` · used by shop_members.shop_members_55_revoke_calendar_feed — Deactivation revokes the member's live feed token (see the header). AFTER UPDATE OF active on shop_members, every context (definer: the tokens are RPC-only). Serialized with create_calendar_feed by the same advisory lock, so a token made concurrently cannot outlive the removal.
- `time_entries_geo_guard()` — invoker · `0056_sched_time_geo.sql` · used by time_entries.time_entries_50_geo_guard

## Money v2 (0060–0069): terminal, multi-job invoices, tips & commissions, gift cards, fees, proposals

### Enums

- `commission_kind`: none | percent | flat  _(0060_money_enums.sql)_ — services.commission_kind: none | percent (commission_value in bps of the line net) | flat (commission_value cents per unit).
- `fee_apply_location`: none | shop | mobile | both  _(0060_money_enums.sql)_ — shop_fees.auto_apply: none (added by hand) | shop | mobile | both (location types that get the fee automatically).
- `gift_card_status`: active | depleted | void | expired  _(0060_money_enums.sql)_ — gift_cards.status: active | depleted (balance 0) | void | expired.
- `gift_card_txn_kind`: issue | redeem | refund | adjust | void | expire  _(0060_money_enums.sql)_ — gift_card_transactions.kind: issue | redeem | refund (credit back or order refund) | adjust | void | expire.

### Tables

#### `coupon_redemptions`

file `0061_money_schema.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> One row per job that carries a coupon (jobs_zz_money_coupon_redemption, every context). once_per_customer is copied from the coupon when redeemed and holds the customer's single use (unique per coupon and customer); it is cleared while the job is cancelled / no-show (0121). Server-maintained; managers+ read.

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| coupon_id | uuid | no |  |
| customer_id | uuid | no |  |
| job_id | uuid | no |  |
| once_per_customer | boolean | no |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (job_id); (shop_id, id); coupon_redemptions_once_key (coupon_id, customer_id) WHERE once_per_customer
- FK `coupon_redemptions_coupon_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, coupon_id) REFERENCES coupons(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `coupon_redemptions_customer_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `coupon_redemptions_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `coupon_redemptions_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S
- Policies:

- FK `referral_credits_card_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, gift_card_id) REFERENCES gift_cards(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (gift_card_id)
- FK `referral_credits_coupon_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, coupon_id) REFERENCES coupons(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (coupon_id)
- FK `referral_credits_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (job_id)
- FK `referral_credits_referee_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, referee_customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `referral_credits_referrer_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, referrer_customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `referral_credits_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `referral_credits_amount_cents_check` (amount_cents >= 0); `referral_credits_skipped_zero` (status = 'issued' OR amount_cents = 0); `referral_credits_status_check` (status = ANY (ARRAY['issued', 'skipped']))
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S
- Policies:
  - `referral_credits_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `referral_credits_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change()

#### `referral_settings`

file `0061_money_schema.sql` · also altered/policies in `0069_money_memberships_referrals.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Referral program (P-29): the new customer's discount (their referral coupon) and the referrer's store credit once the referee's first job is completed. Nothing enabled or seeded with amounts. Managers read, owners/admins edit.

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| enabled | boolean | no | false |
| referee_discount_kind | coupon_kind | no | 'fixed'::coupon_kind |
| referee_discount_value | bigint | no | 0 |
| referrer_reward_cents | bigint | no | 0 |
| terms | text | yes |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |

- PK: (shop_id)
- FK `referral_settings_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `referral_settings_enabled_discount` (NOT enabled OR referee_discount_value > 0); `referral_settings_percent` (referee_discount_kind <> 'percent'::coupon_kind OR referee_discount_value <= 10000); `referral_settings_referee_discount_value_check` (referee_discount_value >= 0); `referral_settings_referrer_reward_cents_check` (referrer_reward_cents >= 0); `referral_settings_terms_check` (terms IS NULL OR char_length(terms) <= 2000)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SU
- Policies:
  - `referral_settings_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
  - `referral_settings_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_admin(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_admin(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `referral_settings_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `referral_settings_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at(); `referral_settings_zz_money_sync` AFTER UPDATE OF enabled, referee_discount_kind, referee_discount_value ROW → referral_settings_money_sync()

#### `gift_card_order_paid`

`gift_card_order_paid(p_order_id uuid, p_payment_intent_id text, p_amount_received_cents bigint)` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0066_money_gift_cards.sql`
- gift_card_order_paid(order, payment intent, amount received) — service_role (stripe-webhook). Idempotent: a paid order (or an intent that already issued a card) returns {first_time: false} and issues nothing. The amount must be the order's price (22023). Issues the card (online, expiry per settings), matches / creates the purchaser and the recipient as customers (leads, no consent), queues gift_card_delivery to the recipient (and a copy to the purchaser when their email differs) and notifies managers (gift_card_purchased). Messaging failures never undo the card. Returns {gift_card_id, last4, first_time}.
- Called by:
  - edge:stripe-webhook: `supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/handlers.ts`

#### `gift_card_order_refunded`

`gift_card_order_refunded(p_payment_intent_id text, p_refunded_total_cents bigint)` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0066_money_gift_cards.sql`
- gift_card_order_refunded(payment intent, refunded total) — service_role (stripe-webhook charge.refunded on a gift card sale). p_refunded_total_cents is the charge's cumulative refund (price terms); it only ever grows. The matching card value (refund × value / price, cumulative rounding) comes off the unspent balance — never below zero; what was already spent is reported as unrecovered_cents. A fully refunded, never-used card is voided. Returns {gift_card_id, refunded_total_cents, removed_cents, unrecovered_cents, status}.
- Called by:
  - edge:stripe-webhook: `supabase/functions/stripe-webhook/handlers.ts`

#### `membership_period_bounds`

`membership_period_bounds(p_membership_id uuid, p_at timestamp with time zone)` → `tstzrange`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0069_money_memberships_referrals.sql`, `0111_money_membership_paid_through.sql`
- The billing period containing p_at. Periods are whole intervals counted FORWARD from a billing anchor A — [A + k·I, A + (k+1)·I), UTC calendar arithmetic from A itself — which is how Stripe computes them: a subscription anchored on the 31st renews on Feb 28, Mar 31, Apr 30, so the April period is [Mar 31, Apr 30), not [Mar 30, Apr 30) (subtracting months from a clamped period end would move the start up to 3 days). The anchor: * started_at when current_period_end is one of its boundaries (Stripe's default: the billing cycle is anchored on the subscription's start_date, which the webhook passes as started_at), so every renewal keeps the same periods and visits already booked stay in theirs; * else current_period_end (the anchor moved: a trial, a plan change that reset the cycle, a backdated start): [end − I, end) is the current period and the others step from it (exact unless that end itself fell on a shortened month end); * without a period end (not billed by Stripe yet): started_at, else created_at.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `jobs_money_membership_uses`, `membership_usage`, `membership_uses_in_period`

#### `public_schedule_quote`

`public_schedule_quote(p_token uuid, p_starts_at text, p_location jsonb DEFAULT NULL::jsonb, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: anon, authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0067_money_quotes_v2.sql`
- public_schedule_quote(token, starts_at, location, now) — anon / signed-in: books the approved quote at one of public_quote_slots' starts (starts_at as in create_online_booking: ISO-8601; without an offset it is shop-local wall time). location {type, address_line1*, address_line2, city*, region, postal_code*} (* for mobile, inside the service area). Serialized with online bookings (same advisory lock), the exact slot is re-validated, the quote converts to a job ('scheduled' with auto-confirm, else 'requested') with the shop's deposit rule, staff are notified and the customer gets the booking confirmation / request message. Returns {job_token, job_number, status, total_cents, deposit_required_cents, deposit_due_cents}. 23P01 slot taken; 22023 not schedulable / invalid input; PT404 unknown token.
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/public-docs/api.ts`

## Field ops v2 (0070–0079): job reports, remote sign-off, required checklists, documents, merge

### Enums

- `inventory_movement_kind`: receive | consume | adjust | count  _(0070_ops_enums.sql)_

### Tables

#### `documents`

file `0071_ops_schema.sql` · also altered/policies in `0075_ops_documents.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Files (PDF, Word, Excel, text, images) on customers and jobs, stored in the documents bucket: <shop_id>/customers/<customer_id>/<file> or <shop_id>/jobs/<job_id>/<file>.

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| customer_id | uuid | yes |  | The customer the file belongs to. For job files it is always the job's customer (filled by the server). |
| job_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| storage_path | text | no |  |  |
| file_name | text | no |  |  |
| content_type | text | no |  |  |
| size_bytes | bigint | no |  |  |
| customer_visible | boolean | no | false | Shown to the customer on the job report, booking page and client portal. |
| uploaded_by | uuid | yes |  |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id); (shop_id, storage_path)
- FK `documents_customer_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, customer_id) REFERENCES customers(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `documents_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `documents_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `documents_uploaded_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (uploaded_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- CHECK: `documents_content_type_check` (char_length(content_type) >= 1 AND char_length(content_type) <= 120); `documents_file_name_check` (char_length(btrim(file_name)) >= 1 AND char_length(btrim(file_name)) <= 255); `documents_has_owner` (customer_id IS NOT NULL OR job_id IS NOT NULL); `documents_size_bytes_check` (size_bytes >= 0 AND size_bytes <= 26214400); `documents_storage_path_check` (is_safe_storage_path(storage_path))
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=SID + cols UPDATE(file_name, customer_visible)
- Policies:
  - `documents_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `(is_shop_manager(shop_id) OR ((uploaded_by = auth.uid()) AND (job_id IS NOT NULL) AND can_work_job(shop_id, job_id)))`
  - `documents_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `(is_shop_manager(shop_id) OR ((job_id IS NOT NULL) AND can_work_job(shop_id, job_id)))`
  - `documents_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `(is_shop_manager(shop_id) OR ((job_id IS NOT NULL) AND can_work_job(shop_id, job_id)))`
  - `documents_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)` — CHECK `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `documents_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change(); `documents_10_client_guard` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → documents_client_guard(); `documents_20_fill_customer` BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF job_id, customer_id ROW → documents_fill_customer(); `documents_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at(); `documents_zz_ops_queue_storage_purge` AFTER DELETE ROW → documents_queue_storage_purge(); `documents_zz_ops_validate` AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ROW → documents_validate()

#### `inventory_movements`

file `0071_ops_schema.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Inventory ledger. quantity is signed (receive +, consume -, adjust +/-, count = the delta to the counted level). Written only by record_inventory_movement and job completion.

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |  |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| product_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| kind | inventory_movement_kind | no |  |  |
| quantity | numeric(12,3) | no |  |  |
| unit_cost_cents | bigint | yes |  | receive: purchase cost per unit (updates the product cost); consume: the product cost when the materials were consumed (job completion, or a line added to a completed job). |
| job_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| allocation | jsonb | yes |  | consume: {"<line service_id>": quantity, ...} — how much of this product each service of the job used (job/service profit). |
| note | text | yes |  |  |
| created_by | uuid | yes |  |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, id)
- FK `inventory_movements_created_by_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `inventory_movements_job_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, job_id) REFERENCES jobs(shop_id, id) ON DELETE SET NULL (job_id)
- FK `inventory_movements_product_fk`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id, product_id) REFERENCES products(shop_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- FK `inventory_movements_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `inventory_movements_allocation` (allocation IS NULL OR kind = 'consume'::inventory_movement_kind AND jsonb_typeof(allocation) = 'object'); `inventory_movements_job_consume` (kind = 'consume'::inventory_movement_kind OR job_id IS NULL); `inventory_movements_note_check` (note IS NULL OR char_length(note) <= 500); `inventory_movements_sign` ( CASE kind WHEN 'receive'::inventory_movement_kind THEN quantity > 0::numeric WHEN 'consume'::inventory_movement_kind THEN quantity < 0::numeric WHEN 'adjust'::inventory_movement_kind THEN quantity <> 0::numeric ELSE t…; `inventory_movements_unit_cost_cents_check` (unit_cost_cents IS NULL OR unit_cost_cents >= 0)
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=S
- Policies:
  - `inventory_movements_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `inventory_movements_05_prevent_shop_change` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → prevent_shop_change()

#### `booking_document_media`

`booking_document_media(p_token uuid)` → `TABLE(ref_id uuid, bucket text, path text)`

#### `comms_inbound_sms_customer`

`comms_inbound_sms_customer(p_shop_id uuid, p_phone text)` → `customers`

- **DEFINER** · sql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0074_ops_merge_customers.sql`
- comms_inbound_sms_customer (new here; service_role only, see Grants) — the customer an inbound text belongs to, following merges. The duplicate keeps its phone when the survivor has its own, so a text from the duplicate's number is matched to the duplicate and then followed along merged_into_id to the customer that survived (a survivor may itself have been merged later). Order: a customer who holds the number and is active, then the active survivor of a merged holder, then the rest (archived), newest holder first. The survivor of a merge is the same person, so the message, its thread (inbox_threads), the reply and the staff notification all land on the live record.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `record_inbound_sms`

#### `revoke_job_report`

`revoke_job_report(p_report_id uuid)` → `void`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0072_ops_job_reports.sql`
- revoke_job_report — manager+. The link stops working; files a customer uploaded to the report's signature folder but never submitted are queued for the storage purge (a signed inspection keeps its image: in use).
- Called by:

  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/JobsReportService.swift`

- Called by:

  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/JobOpsService.swift`

#### `set_job_status`

`set_job_status(p_job_id uuid, p_status job_status, p_force boolean DEFAULT false, p_reason text DEFAULT NULL::text)` → `jobs`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0073_ops_completion_gates.sql`
- set_job_status — the status RPC (every role, same rules as a direct update), with the manager override of the completion gates. p_force managers+ only (42501 otherwise); when the gates would block, the waived blockers are recorded in job_gate_overrides. p_reason the override reason (<= 500 characters); a move to cancelled also stores it as the job's cancel_reason. Returns the job (public_token null: the customer's credential).
- Called by:
  - web: `web/src/features/jobs/api.ts`
  - iOS: `ios/DetailCRM/DetailCRM/Core/Services/JobService.swift`

## Comms & integrations v2 (0080–0089): push, reminders, import/export, custom fields, lead forms

### Enums

- `custom_field_entity`: customer | job  _(0080_comms_enums.sql)_ — custom_fields.entity: customer (customers.custom_data, lead forms) | job (jobs.custom_data, booking questions).
- `custom_field_type`: text | textarea | number | select | multiselect | checkbox | date  _(0080_comms_enums.sql)_ — custom_fields.type: text (<= 2000) | textarea (<= 10000) | number | select (one option) | multiselect (options) | checkbox (boolean) | date (YYYY-MM-DD).

### Tables

#### `custom_fields`

| key | text | no |  |
| label | text | no |  |

| options | text[] | no | '{}'::text[] |
| help_text | text | yes |  |
| required | boolean | no | false |
| show_in_booking | boolean | no | false |
| show_in_lead_form | boolean | no | false |
| location_scope | location_type | yes |  |
| sort | integer | no | 0 |
| archived_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |

- PK: (id)
- UNIQUE: (shop_id, entity, key); (shop_id, id)

- CHECK: `custom_fields_booking_job` (NOT show_in_booking OR entity = 'job'::custom_field_entity); `custom_fields_help_text_check` (help_text IS NULL OR char_length(help_text) <= 300); `custom_fields_key_check` (key ~ '^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,39}$'); `custom_fields_label_check` (char_length(btrim(label)) >= 1 AND char_length(btrim(label)) <= 120); `custom_fields_lead_form_customer` (NOT show_in_lead_form OR entity = 'customer'::custom_field_entity); `custom_fields_location_scope_job` (location_scope IS NULL OR entity = 'job'::custom_field_entity); `custom_fields_options` (comms_custom_field_options_valid(type, options))

#### `comms_deposit_due_cents`

`comms_deposit_due_cents(p_job_id uuid)` → `bigint`

- invoker · sql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0085_comms_document_followups.sql`
- The deposit a job still needs, in cents: the required deposit (capped at the total: the invoice's once the job has one) minus what was received on the job and what is still in flight (a processing ACH debit is not chased) — and, once the job is billed on a live invoice (single-job or grouped, found through invoice_jobs), never more than that invoice still needs (comms_invoice_due_cents). A grouped (fleet) invoice's payments carry no job_id, so without that cap every job of a fully paid grouped invoice would still be asked for its deposit. Same basis as the /booking page's deposit.due_cents (booking_public_json, 0064), with money in flight counted as paid.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `comms_followup_candidates`, `comms_withdraw_reason`, `enqueue_document_followups`, `payments_hold_job_checkout`

#### `comms_followup_due_at`

`comms_followup_due_at(p_base_at timestamp with time zone, p_first interval, p_repeat interval, p_in_days boolean, p_timezone text, p_attempt integer)` → `timestamp with time zone`

- invoker · sql · immutable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0085_comms_document_followups.sql`
- When attempt p_attempt of a schedule is due. Hour schedules are elapsed time from p_base_at (p_base_at + p_first + (p_attempt - 1) * p_repeat), moved into waking hours: a time before 08:00 shop-local becomes 08:00 that morning, one from 21:00 on becomes 08:00 the next morning (attempts that land in the same night fall due together, and the run sends only the latest of them). Day schedules (p_first / p_repeat whole days) count shop-local calendar days from the local date of p_base_at and are due at 10:00 local time (a waking hour; a DST change never shifts it). When attempt 1's 10:00 would not be after p_base_at (first = 0 days, or a base time after 10:00 on that date), the whole schedule moves one day later, so no attempt is due before the base (e.g. before the invoice is overdue) and attempts stay p_repeat apart.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `comms_followup_status_json`, `enqueue_document_followups`, `enqueue_service_followups`

#### `comms_followup_status_json`

`comms_followup_status_json(p_kind text, p_id uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone)` → `jsonb`

- invoker · plpgsql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0085_comms_document_followups.sql`
- Staff view / pause of one document's follow-ups (owner/admin/manager). p_kind 'quote' (quote id) | 'deposit' (job id) | 'invoice' (invoice id) Result {kind, stage, enabled, paused, attempts_sent, max_attempts, last_sent_at, next_at}: stage = the schedule in effect ('quote', 'deposit', 'invoice' or — once the invoice is past due — 'invoice_overdue'); enabled = the shop's setting is on and at least one channel template of the key is enabled; attempts_sent / last_sent_at = attempts that queued a message; next_at = when the next attempt is due (null when none will be sent: off, paused, no attempts left, or the document no longer qualifies). Unknown / other shop's document: P0002; technicians: 42501.
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `document_followup_status`, `set_document_followups_paused`

#### `comms_grouped_invoice_job_amounts`

`comms_grouped_invoice_job_amounts(p_invoice_id uuid)` → `TABLE(job_id uuid, total_cents bigint, paid_cents bigint, balance_cents bigint)`

- invoker · sql · stable · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0087_comms_import_export.sql`
- comms_grouped_invoice_job_amounts(invoice) — INTERNAL (service_role and definer code). A grouped invoice's amounts split per job, so that summing the rows of its jobs gives the invoice's own figures exactly (a CSV that repeated the invoice-wide amounts on every job would count a fleet invoice once per job): total_cents each job's own total, in job order (appointment, then number); the last job takes whatever the invoice total differs by (tax rounded once on the invoice, lines edited on the invoice), and no job gets more than is left of it; paid_cents the invoice's received money (amount_paid_cents, net of refunds, tips excluded): first each job's own payments (a deposit taken on the job, carried onto the invoice), up to its total; the rest — whole-invoice payments, which carry no job_id — fills the jobs in the same order, the last job taking any overpayment; balance_cents total_cents − paid_cents (sums to the invoice balance).
- Called by:
  - SQL functions: `export_jobs`

- Does a reminder log row stand for the reminder at offset p_offset (due at p_due_at)? A row logged for that offset does. A row without an offset (a single-reminder shop, or logged before P-4) is THE reminder while the shop has a single reminder; once it has several, it stands for every offset that was due by the time it was processed (a reminder sent a day before covers the "1 day before" offset, not the "1 hour before" one).

### Trigger functions

- `custom_fields_guard()` — **DEFINER** · `0088_comms_custom_fields_leads_tracking.sql` · used by custom_fields.custom_fields_80_guard — Definitions with saved values (customers.custom_data / jobs.custom_data, and for customer fields lead_submissions.answers) keep their identity: no delete (archive instead), no key / entity / type change. A deleted field leaves the lead forms that asked it.
- `custom_fields_normalize()` — invoker · `0088_comms_custom_fields_leads_tracking.sql` · used by custom_fields.custom_fields_80_normalize
- `customers_comms_custom_data()` — **DEFINER** · `0088_comms_custom_fields_leads_tracking.sql` · used by customers.customers_85_custom_data
- `customers_comms_merge_follow()` — **DEFINER** · `0088_comms_custom_fields_leads_tracking.sql` · used by customers.customers_zz_comms_merge_follow — Customer merge (ops 0074 sets customers.merged_into_id on the duplicate after moving its records): the comms rows follow — lead submissions and tasks move to the surviving customer, and custom data the survivor lacks is copied from the duplicate (only keys of live customer fields whose value is still valid; the survivor's own values always win).
- `integration_events_comms_webhooks()` — **DEFINER** · `0089_comms_sms_webhooks.sql` · used by integration_events.integration_events_zz_comms_webhooks — AFTER INSERT ON integration_events: one pending delivery per active, subscribed endpoint of the shop. Never blocks the business write.
- `job_assignments_comms_notify()` — **DEFINER** · `0082_comms_push.sql` · used by job_assignments.job_assignments_zz_comms_notify — Job assigned: the assigned member gets "New job #N" when a signed-in staff member assigned them (not themselves; system writes by service_role / cron announce nothing). Occurrences a recurring series generates in bulk (series_seq > 1, created in the same transaction) are not announced one by one: the first occurrence is; likewise a series edit updating its occurrences in place announces only the…
- `jobs_comms_custom_data()` — **DEFINER** · `0088_comms_custom_fields_leads_tracking.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_85_custom_data
- `jobs_comms_notify_rescheduled()` — **DEFINER** · `0082_comms_push.sql` · used by jobs.jobs_zz_comms_notify_rescheduled — Job rescheduled: every assigned member except the staff member who moved it (system writes by service_role / cron announce nothing; a series edit moving its occurrences in place announces only the first one it changes, see detailcrm.series_bulk_edit above).
- `lead_forms_validate()` — **DEFINER** · `0088_comms_custom_fields_leads_tracking.sql` · used by lead_forms.lead_forms_80_validate — lead_forms: field_ids are the shop's CUSTOMER fields (distinct, in order).

## Cross-cutting hardening (0090–0099)

### Tables

#### `client_requests`

file `0095_integration_parity_fixes.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> Idempotency ledger (0095): the result of a request made with a client request nonce (add_fee_line, import_customers / import_services chunks). Definer code only; pruned after 7 days.

| column | type | null | default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | no | gen_random_uuid() |
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |
| user_id | uuid | yes |  |
| kind | text | no |  |
| nonce | text | no |  |

#### `replace_business_hours`

- replace_business_hours — replaces all of a shop's business hours in one statement block (owner/admin). p_rows: a JSON array (at most 50) of {"weekday": 0-6 (0 = Sunday), "opens_at": "HH:MM[:SS]", "closes_at": "HH:MM[:SS]" (24:00 allowed)}; [] = closed all week. Overlapping intervals (23P01) or closes_at <= opens_at (23514) fail the whole call, so the previous hours stay. Concurrent saves of one shop are serialized by an advisory lock. Returns the stored rows by weekday, time.

Same rules as the customer's own public_respond_quote: only sent / viewed quotes inside their validity. 'approve' p_selected_optional_line_ids (when not null) must be optional lines of this quote and becomes exactly the selected set; null keeps the current selections. approved_by_name = the name the customer gave (optional, ≤ 200). 'decline' declined_reason (optional, ≤ 1000). Returns the updated quote.

#### `shop_billing`

file `0100_billing_schema.sql` · RLS on · realtime: no

> A shop's platform subscription (Stripe platform account). Written by definer code / service_role only (billing-webhook -> 0101 RPCs). Owners, admins and managers read the non-Stripe columns; everyone else uses shop_entitlement().

| column | type | null | default | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| shop_id | uuid | no |  |  |
| stripe_customer_id | text | yes |  |  |
| stripe_subscription_id | text | yes |  |  |
| plan_id | uuid | yes |  |  |
| status | text | no | 'none' | Stripe subscription status (none = never subscribed). The shop's standing is derived by billing_state (0101). |
| trial_ends_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  | End of the shop's trial: the in-app trial (set_billing_config / new shops) or the Stripe subscription's trial_end. |
| trial_used | boolean | no | false | A Stripe subscription of this shop had a trial: checkout no longer carries the in-app trial over. |
| current_period_end | timestamp with time zone | yes |  | Stripe's current period end of the subscription. Stripe moves it on at renewal BEFORE the renewal is paid, so it is never what an ended subscription keeps access through (paid_through is). |
| paid_through | timestamp with time zone | yes |  | End of the last period the shop is in good standing for (billing_apply_subscription, 0101): the period end while the subscription is active / trialing; capped at the event time once it is past_due /… |
| cancel_at_period_end | boolean | no | false |  |
| comp_until | timestamp with time zone | yes |  | Free until this time ('infinity' = for good): billing_set_comp, the operator's pilot-shop tool. |
| last_event_at | timestamp with time zone | yes |  | Created time of the newest Stripe event applied (older deliveries are ignored). |
| created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |
| updated_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |

- PK: (shop_id)
- UNIQUE: (stripe_customer_id); (stripe_subscription_id)
- FK `shop_billing_plan_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (plan_id) REFERENCES platform_plans(id) ON DELETE SET NULL
- FK `shop_billing_shop_id_fkey`: FOREIGN KEY (shop_id) REFERENCES shops(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
- CHECK: `shop_billing_status_check` (status = ANY (ARRAY['none', 'trialing', 'active', 'past_due', 'canceled', 'unpaid', 'incomplete', 'incomplete_expired', 'paused'])); `shop_billing_stripe_customer_id_check` (stripe_customer_id IS NULL OR stripe_customer_id ~ '^cus_[A-Za-z0-9]+$'); `shop_billing_stripe_subscription_id_check` (stripe_subscription_id IS NULL OR stripe_subscription_id ~ '^sub_[A-Za-z0-9]+$')
- Grants: anon=—; authenticated=— + cols SELECT(shop_id, plan_id, status, trial_ends_at, trial_used, current_period_end, cancel_at_period_end, comp_until, created_at, updated_at)
- Policies:
  - `shop_billing_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `is_shop_manager(shop_id)`
- Triggers: `shop_billing_90_set_updated_at` BEFORE UPDATE ROW → set_updated_at()

## Other (0101)

### Functions

#### `billing_apply_subscription`

`billing_apply_subscription(p_stripe_customer_id text, p_subscription_id text, p_price_id text, p_status text, p_trial_end timestamp with time zone, p_current_period_end timestamp with time zone, p_cancel_at_period_end boolean, p_event_created timestamp with time zone)` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0101_billing_rpcs.sql`
- billing_apply_subscription — a subscription's CURRENT state (re-read from Stripe by the webhook) as of p_event_created. Returns {shop_id, applied}: * unknown customer: {shop_id: null, applied: false}; * an event older than the newest one applied (last_event_at): nothing changes, {shop_id, applied: false} (out-of-order deliveries never roll a shop back; equal times are applied — the state is re-read anyway); * another subscription than the shop's current one is applied only when it can be the shop's subscription now: one that has ended (canceled / incomplete_expired) never replaces the current one, and a pending (incomplete) one never replaces a live one — {shop_id, applied: false}; * plan: the plan with that price (active or retired); an unknown price leaves plan_id null but the status is still applied; * trial_used once a subscription had a trial (p_trial_end or trialing); trial_ends_at follows the subscription's trial_end when it has one; * canceled (subscription deleted): current_period_end is kept when the event carries none, cancel_at_period_end becomes false; * paid_through (what an ended subscription keeps access through, see the header): active / trialing -> the period end…
- Called by:
  - edge:billing-webhook: `supabase/functions/billing-webhook/handlers.ts`

#### `billing_checkout_context`

Any ACTIVE member; anyone else (and an unknown shop) P0002. {billing_enabled, state, reason, plan_name, trial_ends_at, current_period_end, cancel_at_period_end, max_members, members_used, can_write, is_owner}. Billing details (plan_name, trial_ends_at, current_period_end, cancel_at_period_end, max_members, members_used) are for owners, admins and managers: technicians get null (false) there and only the standing they need to explain a refusal. Never prices or Stripe ids. current_period_end is the renewal / end date of a live subscription; for an ended one (canceled / unpaid / incomplete_expired / paused) it is when access ends or ended (paid_through), never the unpaid period's end.

> Internal (0106): the Stripe Checkout Sessions the payments edge opened for a job (deposit links, the job's invoice pay links) that may still be paid — until expires_at, or until released (expired by the edge, or completed: a processing / received payment row with that session). | created_at | timestamp with time zone | no | now() |  |

### Functions

#### `payments_hold_job_checkout`

`payments_hold_job_checkout(p_shop_id uuid, p_job_id uuid, p_session_id text, p_expires_at timestamp with time zone, p_amount_cents bigint DEFAULT NULL::bigint)` → `void`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0106_public_cancel_checkout_holds.sql`, `0118_money_job_edit_wait_for_open_checkouts.sql`
- (COMMENT ON) service_role (payments edge, 0106 / 0118): record an open Checkout Session of a job until p_expires_at, before handing out its URL. Locks the job; 55000 HINT booking_closed when the job is cancelled / no-show / completed. For a deposit page (a session not held for an invoice) it also locks the job's live invoice(s) and refuses 55000 HINT deposit_not_due (nothing is due any more: comms_deposit_due_cents) or balance_changed (p_amount_cents, the session's amount, exceeds what is due) — the edge then expires the session. Idempotent per session (a repeat only extends the hold).
- Called by:
  - edge:payments: `supabase/functions/payments/checkout_holds.ts`

#### `payments_release_job_checkouts`

`payments_release_job_checkouts(p_shop_id uuid, p_job_id uuid, p_session_ids text[] DEFAULT NULL::text[])` → `integer`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: service_role · search_path="" · file `0106_public_cancel_checkout_holds.sql`, `0109_money_cancel_and_open_checkouts.sql`
- (COMMENT ON) service_role (payments edge, 0106): forget the job's open Checkout Session holds — p_session_ids, or all of them when null — once the edge expired them. Returns how many were released.
- Called by:
  - edge:payments: `supabase/functions/payments/checkout_holds.ts`

#### `job_coupon_eligibility_stale`

`job_coupon_eligibility_stale(p_job_id uuid)` → `boolean`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · stable · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0130_money_restamp_job_coupon.sql`
- (COMMENT ON) Manager+ (0130): the job's coupon covers different lines than its current service list says (a list edit skipped the job while it was settled or its deposit page was open) and the job is not billed — restamp_job_coupon brings it up to date.

#### `restamp_job_coupon`

`restamp_job_coupon(p_job_id uuid)` → `jsonb`

- **DEFINER** · plpgsql · volatile · exec: authenticated, service_role · search_path="" · file `0130_money_restamp_job_coupon.sql`
- (COMMENT ON) Manager+ (0130): "Refresh coupon" — re-stamps the discount eligibility of the job's lines from its current coupon's service list without redeeming the coupon again (so an expired, deactivated or used-up coupon the job already holds can be refreshed). 22023 no coupon; 23514 billed; 55000 HINT checkout_open when a lower total meets an open deposit page. Returns {lines_changed, total_cents}.
- Called by: no statically named web / iOS / edge-function / SQL caller in this repo

## Storage (buckets and `storage.objects` policies)

| bucket | public | size limit | allowed mime types |
|---|---|---|---|
| `documents` | no | 26214400 B (25 MiB) | application/pdf, application/msword, application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document, application/vnd.ms-excel, application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet, text/plain, text/csv, image/jpeg, image/png, image/webp, image/heic |
| `job-media` | no | 209715200 B (200 MiB) | video/mp4, video/quicktime |
| `job-photos` | no | 20971520 B (20 MiB) | image/jpeg, image/png, image/webp, image/heic, image/heif |
| `shop-assets` | yes | 5242880 B (5 MiB) | image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp |
| `signatures` | no | 2097152 B (2 MiB) | image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp |

### Object path conventions

Object names are `/`-separated folders. The layout is the one documented next to the bucket definition; the folder checks are derived from the `storage.objects` policies (`storage_path_uuid(name, N)` passed to a public function names the argument that folder N must satisfy); the columns are the table columns that store object names of the bucket (from their column comments or the trigger that requires the object).

#### `documents`

- Layout: not documented in a migration
- Folder 1: `shop_id` of `can_work_job`, `is_shop_manager`
- Folder 3: `job_id` of `can_work_job`
- Whole-name checks: `is_safe_storage_path`
- Stored in: `storage_purge_requests.path` (rows whose `bucket_id` is this bucket)

#### `job-media`

- Layout: not documented in a migration
- Folder 1: `shop_id` of `can_work_job`, `is_shop_manager`, `is_shop_member`
- Folder 2: `job_id` of `can_work_job`
- Whole-name checks: `is_safe_storage_path`
- Stored in: `storage_purge_requests.path` (rows whose `bucket_id` is this bucket)

#### `job-photos`

- Layout: `<shop_id>/<job_id>/<file>` _(documented in `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`)_
- Folder 1: `shop_id` of `can_work_job`, `is_shop_manager`, `is_shop_member`
- Folder 2: `job_id` of `can_work_job`
- Whole-name checks: `is_safe_storage_path`, `is_signed_evidence`
- Stored in: `inspection_marks.photo_path`, `job_photos.poster_path`, `job_photos.storage_path`, `storage_purge_requests.path` (rows whose `bucket_id` is this bucket)

#### `shop-assets`

- Layout: `<shop_id>/...` _(documented in `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`)_
- Folder 1: `shop_id` of `is_shop_admin`, `is_shop_manager`, `is_shop_member`
- Whole-name checks: `is_safe_storage_path`
- Stored in: `services.image_path`, `shops.logo_path`, `storage_purge_requests.path` (rows whose `bucket_id` is this bucket)

#### `signatures`

- Layout: `<shop_id>/...` _(documented in `0025_field_ops_storage.sql`)_
- Folder 1: `shop_id` of `is_shop_manager`, `is_shop_member`
- Whole-name checks: `can_read_signature_object`, `is_safe_storage_path`, `is_signed_evidence`, `public_form_signature_upload_allowed`, `public_report_signature_upload_allowed`
- Stored in: `form_submissions.signature_path`, `inspections.customer_signature_path`, `storage_purge_requests.path` (rows whose `bucket_id` is this bucket)

Other object-path columns (no bucket named by the schema): `documents.storage_path`, `profiles.avatar_path`

storage.objects policies:
- `field_ops_job_photos_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `field_ops_shop_assets_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND (is_shop_admin(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'services') AND (split_part(name, '/', 3) <> '') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_signatures_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `ops_documents_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'documents') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND (split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'jobs') AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 3)))))`
- `ops_job_media_delete` DELETE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-media') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_job_photos_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `field_ops_shop_assets_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (is_shop_admin(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'services') AND (split_part(name, '/', 3) <> '') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_signatures_insert_public_form` INSERT to anon,authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND public_form_signature_upload_allowed(name))`
- `field_ops_signatures_insert_staff` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `ops_documents_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'documents') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (cardinality(string_to_array(name, '/')) = 4) AND (storage_path_uuid(name, 3) IS NOT NULL) AND (((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'customers') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1))) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'jobs') AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 3)))))`
- `ops_job_media_insert` INSERT to authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'job-media') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (cardinality(string_to_array(name, '/')) = 3) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)))`
- `ops_signatures_insert_public_report` INSERT to anon,authenticated — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND public_report_signature_upload_allowed(name))`
- `field_ops_job_photos_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_shop_assets_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))`
- `field_ops_signatures_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND (can_read_signature_object(name) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `ops_documents_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'documents') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'jobs') AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 3)))))`
- `ops_job_media_select` SELECT to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-media') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND is_shop_member(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_job_photos_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)))) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))` — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'job-photos') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)))) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `field_ops_shop_assets_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND (is_shop_admin(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'services') AND (split_part(name, '/', 3) <> '') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))` — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'shop-assets') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (is_shop_admin(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR ((split_part(name, '/', 2) = 'services') AND (split_part(name, '/', 3) <> '') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))))`
- `field_ops_signatures_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))` — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'signatures') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) AND (NOT is_signed_evidence(bucket_id, name)))`
- `ops_documents_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'documents') AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))` — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'documents') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (cardinality(string_to_array(name, '/')) = 4) AND (split_part(name, '/', 2) = ANY (ARRAY['customers', 'jobs'])) AND (storage_path_uuid(name, 3) IS NOT NULL) AND is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)))`
- `ops_job_media_update` UPDATE to authenticated — USING `((bucket_id = 'job-media') AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)))))` — CHECK `((bucket_id = 'job-media') AND is_safe_storage_path(name) AND (cardinality(string_to_array(name, '/')) = 3) AND (is_shop_manager(storage_path_uuid(name, 1)) OR (((owner_id = (auth.uid())::text) OR (owner = auth.uid())) AND can_work_job(storage_path_uuid(name, 1), storage_path_uuid(name, 2)))))`

## Realtime publication `supabase_realtime`

- `public.jobs` — subscribers only receive rows their RLS SELECT policies allow
- `public.messages` — subscribers only receive rows their RLS SELECT policies allow
- `public.notifications` — subscribers only receive rows their RLS SELECT policies allow
- `public.payments` — subscribers only receive rows their RLS SELECT policies allow
- `public.tasks` — subscribers only receive rows their RLS SELECT policies allow
- `public.time_entries` — subscribers only receive rows their RLS SELECT policies allow
